Fuel and non-fuel spending lift

### Page 2

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
Heatmap of monthly spending categories
The Westpac DataX Consumer Panel  shows average
consumer spending remained resilient in Q3, rising 1.5%
despite a further lift in fuel costs. Fuel contributed 0.3ppts
to quarterly growth, but discretionary spending did more

of the heavy lifting, contributing 0.9ppts as recreation
and travel-related spending recovered. Households also
continued to add to their financial buffers: savings up 1.3%
in the quarter and average buffers across households with
a mortgage increasing to 22.2 months of repayments.
Beneath the aggregate picture, lower-buffer households
remain more exposed to higher fuel costs and mortgage
repayments, but their discretionary spending share is
looking increasingly similar to other households.

Spending holds up despite higher fuel costs
Average household spending increased 1.5% in Q3, with much
of the strength again driven by transport-related spending.
Fuel contributed 0.3ppts to quarterly growth, while transport
excluding fuel contributed a further 0.4ppts. Excluding the
direct effects of the price-driven lift in fuel, average spend
was up 1.2%qtr. Importantly, stronger fuel spending did not
come at the expense of discretionary purchases. Discretionary

categories contributed 0.9ppts to quarterly spending growth,
compared with 0.5ppts from non-fuel essentials.
Solid rise in non-fuel spend and financial buffers
Discretionary spending dominates
Recreation and culture contributed 0.3ppts to growth, reflecting
stronger spending on sporting services, while restaurants and
hotels added a further 0.2ppts. Transport spending was also
boosted by a rebound in airline expenditure, which more than
unwound the weakness recorded in Q2 when refunds relating

to cancellations due to conflict in the Middle East weighed on
the category. Discretionary spending rose 0.9%qtr, following a
flat result and a 0.1% rise in the previous two quarters.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS  
Savings growth by mortgage status
Households continue to rebuild savings
Savings increased 1.3% in Q3, with the strongest gains again 
recorded among younger households. Savings balances for 
18–34 year olds increased 2.0%qtr, compared with 0.7% 
for those aged 55 and over. Mortgagors also continued to 
accumulate savings more rapidly, recording a 2.8% increase 
compared with 0.7% among non-mortgagors. Savings growth 
among mortgagors has now outpaced non-mortgagors for 
three consecutive years.
Stronger savings have continued to lift mortgage buffers. 
Average buffers (savings divided by the sum of essential 
expenses and minimum mortgage repayments), increased to 
22.2 months of repayments in September. Looking within the 
unit-record sample, median mortgage buffers jumped from 
6.3 months in August to 6.8 months, its highest level since the 
series began in 2019.

Median mortgage buffers
Fuel spending a clear drag on consumers
Fuel spending as a share of total spending
Spending outcomes continued to vary across age groups. 
The squeeze remains most apparent among younger 
households. Fuel spending rose 5.1%qtr for 18–34 year olds, 
against a 1.5%qtr increase in non-fuel spending. By contrast, 
non-fuel spending increased 2.1%qtr among households aged 
55 and over. 
The gap between mortgagors and non-mortgagors was 
more modest. Spending rose 1.6%qtr among mortgagors 
and 1.9%qtr among those without a mortgage, suggesting 
mortgage status alone masks considerable variation in 
household financial positions – a divide that becomes clearer 
when households are grouped by the size of their mortgage 
buffers (see discussion further on in this note). Note that ‘non 
mortgagors’ covers a diverse range of consumers including 
those that are renting and those that own their homes without 
a mortgage.

### Page 4

Past performance is not a reliable indicator of future performance. The forecasts given above are predictive in character. Whilst every effort has been taken to ensure that the assumptions on which the forecasts are based are reasonable, 
the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
4
WESTPAC ECONOMICS  
Median litres of fuel consumed
More importantly, their consumption appears to be less 
responsive to price movements. During periods of cheaper 
fuel, high-buffer households show a noticeably larger lift in 
litres purchased, consistent with stockpiling when prices are 
favourable. Low-buffer households show much less of this 
response, while their fuel volumes have also adjusted less 
during the recent increase in fuel costs.
The spending data show the consequence. Fuel expenditure 
has risen sharply across all three groups recently, but remains 
highest among low-buffer households, where it also absorbs 
a larger share of total spending. Food spending has also 
declined since 2023. This may partly reflect households 
economising within the category, including switching towards 
cheaper or home-brand products, although further work is 
needed to test this.
Taken together, the households with the smallest savings 
cushions face higher mortgage repayments, spend more 
on fuel and appear to have less scope to adjust their fuel 
consumption when prices change. 
Discretionary spend as a share of total
This suggests that in aggregate, household balance sheets 
are looking healthy. The picture is a little at odds with the very 
weak reads on our Westpac-Melbourne Index Consumer 
Sentiment Index which suggests finances are coming under 
much more pressure. The fact that both average spending and 
saving both rose suggests incomes over the quarter were not 
overly stretched and that more of the weakness in sentiment 
may relate to concerns about the outlook (something that may 
also be encouraging consumers to build buffers now, while they 
still can).
Pressures intense for low-buffer households
Aggregate buffer measures mask sizeable differences across 
mortgagors. We can get a sense of these variations by 
combining mortgagors into three broad groups according to 
buffer size, i.e. low, middle and high buffer groups. This shows 
that households with the smallest buffers are also more 
exposed to both interest rates and fuel costs.

The buffer differences are relatively stark, the median savings 
of the low-buffer group just $2k compared to $15k for the 
middle-buffer group and $95k for the high buffer group. 
Low buffer households face median minimum mortgage 
repayments of around $2,600 a month, compared with $2,400 
for the middle buffer group and $2,200 for the highest-buffer 
group. 
Low buffer households also spend more on fuel, at around 
$170 a month compared with $160 for the middle buffer 
group and $150 for the high buffer group.

We can use average pump prices to estimate what this 
expenditure implies for fuel volumes. Low-buffer households 
consume around 70 litres a month, middle-buffer households 
67 litres a month and the high-buffer group around 62 litres. 
This compares to an average of 81, 80 and 70 respectively 
through 2024-25. 
Median fuel spend by customer

### Page 5

Past performance is not a reliable indicator of future performance. The forecasts given above are predictive in character. Whilst every effort has been taken to ensure that the assumptions on which the forecasts are based are reasonable, 
the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
5
WESTPAC ECONOMICS  
Yet despite this greater exposure, the gap in broader spending 
behaviour has narrowed. High-buffer households continue 
to spend more on discretionary categories, averaging around 
$1,940 a month compared with $1,740 for low-buffer 
households. However, their discretionary share has been 
broadly stable since 2023, while the shares of low- and 
middle-buffer households have risen by close to a percentage 
point bringing them more in line with the high-buffer group.

Some of the convergence reflects a longer run down-trend in 
fuel spending that has been more pronounced amongst low 
and middle buffer households. This lowering in fuel spend lifts 
the share available for other categories.

Essential spending excluding fuel, meanwhile, has changed 
little across the three cohorts since 2023. The shift in 
spending patterns has largely occurred on the discretionary 
side.

The result is a somewhat mixed picture. Low-buffer 
households remain considerably more exposed to mortgage 
and fuel costs and have far smaller savings cushions. But their 
discretionary spending has nevertheless moved closer to that 
of better-buffered households, suggesting some recovery in 
spending flexibility despite these constraints. 
Conclusion
Aggregate household finances remain resilient: spending and 
savings both increased in Q3 and mortgage buffers continued 
to rebuild. Discretionary spending has also held up despite the 
sharp increase in fuel costs.

But aggregate resilience masks a much more uneven 
distribution of pressure. Low-buffer households 
simultaneously face higher mortgage repayments, greater fuel 
exposure and much smaller savings cushions. While these 
households have so far maintained discretionary spending, 
their capacity to absorb further pressure is clearly more 
limited.
About the Westpac-DataX Consumer Panel
Developed by DataX, Westpac’s data analytics service for 
institutional and business banking customers, the dataset links 
transaction activity with balance sheet information to give 
a complete view of income, spending, saving and borrowing 
flows. 
The resulting sample of over one million customers provides 
an accurate representation of trends across the wider 
Australian consumer. Monthly observations are available back 
to January 2019 with timely updates provided a few weeks 
after the end of each month. As such, it is perfectly suited to 
quickly tracing the impact and responses to events like interest 
rate moves and energy price shocks.

## Document 341: Following the oil shock, El Niño threatens to hurt macroeconomic resilience in Sub-Saharan Africa further (2026-10-21)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Following the oil shock, El Niño threatens to hurt macroeconomic resilience in Sub-Saharan Africa further (2026-10-21).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-21
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

21 October 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
FOLLOWING THE OIL SHOCK, EL NIÑO THREATENS TO HURT MACROECONOMIC RESILIENCE IN SUB-SAHARAN
AFRICA FURTHER
Lucas Plé
Seven months after the start of the war in the Middle East, which has led to a sharp rise in oil prices, sub-Saharan Africa is holding up well
overall. The deterioration in external accounts and the rise in inflation are more contained than in 2022, during the last oil shock. However,
this resilience is fragile and is eroding as the conflict drags on. Furthermore, an exceptional El Niño event could significantly worsen the

macroeconomic environment in 2027 1.
Economic growth remains robust
For countries with available data, GDP growth held up well in the first half of 2026 2. In mid-October, the IMF revised its regional forecast for
the full year upwards to 4.5% (+0.2 percentage points compared with April), in line with the performance for 2025.
1 El Niño 2026–2027: multiple pressures on prices and supplies
2 1.4% year-on-year (y/y) in South Africa, 4.2% in Nigeria, 5.1% in Kenya, 7% in Angola, 7.3% in Ghana.

Sub-Saharan Africa is highly vulnerable to climate shocks via the agricultural commodities channel

### Page 2

2
The bank for
a changing
world
Chart of the Week / 21 October 2026 economic-research.bnpparibas.com
A mitigated shock on terms of trade
High prices for export commodities (gold, copper, platinum) have helped to cushion the impact on terms of trade and the external
accounts of hydrocarbon-importing countries. For the 10 countries in our sample (see heatmap), the depreciation of their currencies
against the US dollar since last February has been moderate overall (2.3% on average).

Inflation has started to rise again, but it is still being contained so far
In the ten countries in our sample, although inflation is rising again, it is lower year-on-year for the first eight months of 2026 than it was
for the same period in 2022. This is due to:
1. Monetary policies that were still restrictive before the conflict started;
2. Fuel subsidies;
3.   Macroeconomic reforms implemented in recent years (a return to orthodox monetary policy in Nigeria, and efforts towards fiscal

consolidation in many countries);
4. Moderate currency depreciation since February.
As a result, few central banks have tightened their monetary policy since February (South Africa, Uganda and Tanzania).
However, this resilience is still fragile…
On the one hand, governments that have chosen to cap fuel price rises are seeing their public finances deteriorate. In Senegal, energy
subsidies will go above 3% of GDP in 2026, which is unsustainable. On the other hand, the region’s external accounts are exposed to

volatile global commodity prices and tighter international financing conditions.
… and the macroeconomic environment could quickly worsen in 2027, due to an exceptional El Niño event
The region is particularly vulnerable to climate shocks via the agricultural commodities channel. Given agriculture’s high share in its GDP ,
sub-Saharan Africa is more exposed to downside risks to growth than Latin America, Asia or Central Europe (1 st column of the heatmap).

It is also more exposed than other regions of the world to the inflationary effect of rising food prices, given their higher share in the
consumer basket (2 nd column). Finally, even though its dependence on food imports is lower than in Asia and Europe (3 rd column), the
region is highly vulnerable to rising international food commodity prices due to its fragile external accounts. For several countries in our
sample, foreign exchange reserves cover less than four months’ worth of imports of goods and services (Uganda, Tanzania, Ghana and

Zambia), leaving little room for manoeuvre for absorbing a further external shock.
In addition to shocks via the agricultural commodities channel, other localised risks must be considered. In Southern Africa, El Niño is
expected to intensify droughts and threaten electricity generation as a result, particularly in Zambia (where hydropower accounts
for nearly 80% of domestic electricity generation). In East Africa, the increased flooding that El Niño is expected to cause will threaten

infrastructure – particularly logistics infrastructure – which is already less efficient than the global average 3. These two sources of
vulnerability could also increase pressure on growth, prices and the external accounts.
In conclusion, if oil prices remain high and El Niño is exceptionally severe, the region will struggle to absorb this double shock in 2027.
Despite their status as net exporters of hydrocarbons, Nigeria and Angola are also exposed to a deterioration in the macroeconomic

environment in 2027. Only South Africa appears to be better positioned to absorb the shock.
lucas.ple@bnpparibas.com
3 See the World Bank’s Logistics Performance Index.

## Document 348: GLOBAL MARKETS CALL_ Government Bond Yields Ease, Gold Falls

Source PDF: all report pdf/GLOBAL MARKETS CALL_ Government Bond Yields Ease, Gold Falls.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-11-01
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: GLOBAL MARKETS CALL: Government Bond Yields Ease, Gold Falls
Date:    43 minutes ago
GLOBAL MARKETS CALL: Government Bond Yields Ease, Gold Falls

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
GLOBAL MARKETS CALL:
Government Bond Yields Ease,
Gold Falls
By Ed Yardeni & Toby Hearst • 1 Nov 2026
View in browser

### Page 2

Photo by Gaël Gaborel - OrbisTerrae / Unsplash
Investors sought safety in government bonds last week as the AI selloff
rattled stock markets worldwide. Yields eased across most of the major
bond markets. The stock market selloff has been hardest on the markets
that soared the most this year. October is ending with the US well ahead
of the rest of the world. That validates our recent shift to market weight on
the Go Global portfolio strategy. Here’s more:

I. Stock Market Performance
The Stay Home vs Go Global ratios have rebounded sharply since mid-
year, driven by US outperformance (chart). They remain below their early
2025 peaks but are heading back up.
The US MSCI’s share of the All Country World MSCI’s market cap has
risen back to 65.3% (chart). Its share of forward earnings is 54.3%. That
11-point gap is the widest since January 2025. US stocks again are
pricing in far more than their share of global earnings.

### Page 3

October’s biggest losers were this year’s AI winners. South Korea finished
the month at the bottom of the country ETF rankings, down 16.4% in
dollars (chart). Taiwan fell 10.4%. Switzerland topped the board with an
8.8% gain, followed by Mexico (4.8%). The US slipped 0.9%, while the All
Country World ex-US ETF fell 2.9%.
II. Stock Markets Earnings & Valuation

### Page 4

Overseas forward earnings momentum is the strongest in over 15 years.
Forward earnings for the All Country World ex-US MSCI is up 41.7% y/y,
topping its 2021 post-pandemic peak (chart). Forward revenues is up
11.6% y/y. Earnings outpacing revenues by that much implies forward
profit margins are widening significantly.
Japan leads the major markets in forward earnings growth since early
2009 (chart). Its forward earnings has risen 720.7% in yen. The US is up

524.6%. EM forward earnings has surged this year and is now up 224.7%
over the same period. The EMU and the UK have barely doubled.
Valuation multiples are falling even as earnings soar (chart). The US
MSCI’s forward P/E has dropped to 19.1 from 23.7 a year ago. Japan’s is

### Page 5

14.7, and the EMU’s is 13.9. The UK trades at 11.9. EM trades at 8.9, its
lowest since the 2008 financial crisis.
III. Fixed Income
Government bond yields eased last week in most major markets in
response to the AI selloff (chart). The US 10-year Treasury yield has fallen
to 4.91% from 5.00% a week earlier. France’s 10-year government bond
yield has eased to 4.98%. The UK’s 5.30% and Australia’s 5.14% remain
the highest in the group. Japan’s yield has dropped to 2.76%.

IV. Gold
We've been bullish on the gold price since it jumped to a new record high
above $2,000 per ounce in early 2024. We reckoned that buying by the

### Page 6

central banks of countries that feared the US and Europe might freeze
their forex reserves, as happened to Russia after it invaded Ukraine,
which would be bullish for gold. That was true until the war started in the
Middle East on February 28, 2026 (chart).
We aren't sure why gold hasn't bounced off of its key support levels.
Instead, it dropped back below $4,000 last week. The next support is
around $3,400. Rising interest rates may explain why gold has been so

weak if central bank buying is being swamped by selling from private
investors.
The People's Bank of China is still buying gold (chart). Chinese investors
have lost lots of money in their property and stock markets. Now, they
may be bailing out of gold.

### Page 7

Is there still a case for gold to reach a price of $10,000 per ounce by the
end of the decade? Yes, but the case is getting weaker. If the S&P 500
reaches 10,000 by the end of the decade, investors might rebalance their
portfolios by allocating more to gold (chart).
V. Focus On China & Brazil
China’s MSCI stock price index has fallen to 66.4 in dollars, down from
92.0 at its 2025 high (chart). The property bust and deflation continue to
weigh on the market. So does the rapidly aging population.

Chinese tech has missed the AI boom entirely (chart). The China
Technology ETF (CQQQ) has fallen to 37.78, 65.2% below its February
2021 record high. The Nasdaq-100 ETF (QQQ) is 9.5% below its record
high. We continue to favor EM ex-China over China.

### Page 8

In Brazil, President Luiz Inácio Lula da Silva won the October 25 runoff
with nearly 52% of valid votes after trailing Senator Flávio Bolsonaro in the
first round. Investors had positioned for a Bolsonaro win, and Bolsonaro
says he will challenge the result in court. The Brazil MSCI stock price
index fell to 3,508.86 in local currency, giving back most of its post-first-
round gains (chart). It is now up just 1.0% ytd in local currency and 2.2%
in dollars.

## Document 442: US Daily_ Why Has the US Economy Been So Resilient to Higher Rates and Higher Oil Prices_

Source PDF: all report pdf/US Daily_ Why Has the US Economy Been So Resilient to Higher Rates and Higher Oil Prices_.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs Publishing
Date: 2026-09-28
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Abhay Duggirala 
+1(202)585-6760 | 
abhay.duggirala@gs.com 
Goldman Sachs & Co. LLC
US Daily: Why Has the US Economy Been So Resilient to Higher Rates 
and Higher Oil Prices?
28 September 2026 | 10:38AM EDT
We estimate that the economy grew at a 3.4% pace in Q3 and forecast 2.2% n
growth in 2026 overall on a Q4/Q4 basis, close to our estimate of potential GDP 
growth and only 0.3pp below our start-of-the-year forecast before the large 
increases in interest rates and oil prices. Why, we are often asked, has the US 
economy proven so resilient to these shocks? 
While interest rates have risen substantially this year, our financial conditions n
index framework implies that the economic impact should be nearly offset by the 
effects of higher equity prices, narrower credit spreads, and a weaker dollar, 
resulting in only a 0.1pp net hit to 2026 GDP growth. While the net impact of this 
year’s market moves on overall GDP is modest, the effects on some parts of the 
economy—especially the hit to housing—are more meaningful. 
We estimate that the increase in oil and refined product prices this year, together n
with the path our commodities strategists expect through year-end, will subtract 
0.3pp from 2026 growth. The drag comes from consumption as higher energy 
prices erode real purchasing power. While US energy producers were reluctant to 
boost investment when oil prices first rose, the increase in the rig count index in 
recent months points to some offsetting boost to GDP in Q3 and Q4. 
Our analysis suggests that slightly tighter financial conditions and higher oil n
prices should together be roughly a 0.4pp drag on GDP growth. The downgrade 
we have made to our forecast for the year, in part based on incoming data, is only 
slightly smaller than that, with the slight outperformance coming largely from 
consumer spending. This suggests that the economy’s resilience is less puzzling 
than it might appear at first.

### Page 2

Why Has the US Economy Been So Resilient to Higher Rates and Higher Oil Prices?

We now estimate that the economy grew at a 3.4% pace in Q3 and forecast 2.2% growth 
in 2026 overall on a Q4/Q4 basis, close to our 2.3% estimate of potential GDP growth 
and only 0.3pp below our start-of-the-year forecast when we did not expect large 
increases in interest rates and oil prices. In today’s Daily, we ask why the US economy has 
proven so resilient to these surprises. 
Part of the answer is that financial conditions have changed little on net since January, as 
the substantial rise in interest rates has been nearly offset by the effects of higher equity 
prices, narrower credit spreads, and a weaker dollar (Exhibit 1, left). Our estimated GDP 
growth impulse from the FCI is therefore little changed from the start of the year (Exhibit 
1, right), implying only a 0.1pp hit to 2026 Q4/Q4 GDP growth.

While the net impact of this year’s market moves on overall GDP is modest, the effects 
on some components of GDP are more meaningful. We use the Fed’s macroeconomic 
model FRB/US to estimate how the particular combination of market moves this year 
should affect each component of GDP . We find a meaningful hit from higher rates to 
residential investment, offset by slight boosts to consumption and business investment 
(Exhibit 2).

### Page 3

What about the effect of higher oil and refined products prices? Our rule of thumb 
implies that a sustained $10/barrel increase in oil prices lowers GDP growth by just over 
0.1pp through the consumption channel as higher energy costs erode real purchasing 
power. Applied to the path oil prices have taken this year, the unusual widening in 
refined product spreads, and our commodities strategists’ forecasts for prices through 
year-end, the rule of thumb implies a 0.4pp drag on GDP growth. 
While US oil producers were initially reluctant to respond to higher prices by increasing 
capex because of the uncertainty of the war with Iran and the outlook for oil prices, the 
increase in the rig count index in Q3 (Exhibit 3) points to a pickup in energy capex more 
recently. We estimate that the consumption drag and capex boost will net out to a 0.3pp 
hit to GDP growth from the oil shock. Two alternative approaches, FRB/US simulations 
and a model estimated using exogenous oil shocks, deliver similar estimates on average.1 
1 We use the series of exogenous oil shocks from Känzig (2021), “The Macroeconomic Effects of Oil Supply 
News: Evidence from OPEC Announcements” to estimate the effects of the current oil shock on GDP growth.

The Estimated Effect of Realized Financial Conditions 
on Growth in GDP and Its Components

### Page 4

Our analysis suggests that slightly tighter financial conditions and higher oil prices 
should together be roughly a 0.4pp drag on GDP growth. The downgrade we have made 
to our forecast for the year, in part based on incoming data, is only slightly smaller than 
that, suggesting that the puzzle of economic resilience is smaller than it might appear at 
first. 
The economy’s slight outperformance relative to what we might have expected largely 
comes from consumer spending. At the start of the year, we forecasted consumption 
growth of 2.4% in 2026 Q4/Q4. Under the market and commodity price moves that 
actually materialized, our oil shock model implies a 0.6pp hit to that forecast, partially 
offset by a 0.2pp boost from higher equity prices in our wealth effects model, netting to 
an expected 2.0% growth in consumption this year. Against that, we now estimate—in 
part based on the surprisingly solid incoming data in recent months—that consumption 
will grow 2.2%, a modest upside surprise of 0.2pp.

## Document 461: What lessons can we learn to keep European industry competitive from the measures taken by Germany, as the cost of electricity continues to turn the screw_ (2026-10-22)

Source PDF: all report pdf/What lessons can we learn to keep European industry competitive from the measures taken by Germany, as the cost of electricity continues to turn the screw_ (2026-10-22).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

The bank
for a changing
world
WHAT LESSONS CAN WE LEARN TO KEEP EUROPEAN INDUSTRY COMPETITIVE FROM
THE MEASURES TAKEN BY GERMANY, AS THE COST OF ELECTRICITY CONTINUES TO
TURN THE SCREW?
Marianne Mueller and Pascal Devaux
issue 19
21 October 2026
The 2022 energy crisis clearly highlighted the issue of electricity prices in Europe — and, more broadly, the issue of energy prices. As
a result, European manufacturers’ competitiveness has been severely undermined when compared with the United States and China.

By reigniting tensions over electricity prices, the conflict that began in the Middle East in late February 2026 further underscores the
continent’s vulnerability to fossil fuel imports, despite the expansion of renewable energy. Faced with an environment of potential sharp
and sudden price rises, European governments do not have the same room for manoeuvre.
While the additional costs borne by European manufacturers are mainly linked to the share of gas in national electricity mixes, we will

first illustrate that, in Germany, grid operation and maintenance costs, as well as taxation, are also contributing significantly to these
costs.
We will then explore the solutions proposed by the German government under the new European measures regulating state aid to
electricity-intensive industries.
Finally, our estimates will show that, while these measures make Germany more competitive against some European countries, they
do not place it on an equal competitive footing with the United States or China. Nevertheless, Germany has made significant progress

in decarbonising and making its electricity mix more flexible from a European perspective. This should help to ensure that electricity costs
are reduced in the medium term.

### Page 2

2
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoInsight 19 / 21 October 2026
The bank
for a changing
worldWHAT LESSONS CAN WE LEARN TO KEEP EUROPEAN INDUSTRY COMPETITIVE FROM THE MEA-SURES TAKEN BY GERMANY, AS THE COST OF ELECTRICITY CONTINUES TO TURN THE SCREW?
GAS: A KEY FACTOR IN THE ADDITIONAL ELECTRICITY COSTS
FACED BY EUROPEAN INDUSTRY
European manufacturers are still paying significantly higher elec -
tricity costs than their American and Chinese competitors. In 2019,

there was a 30% difference between the prices charged to European
electricity-intensive industries (corresponding to the lower end of the
industrial price range) and the average US industrial price. In 2025,
it stood at nearly 100% according to the IEA (2026) 1, after peaking at
an average of 148% between 2022 and 2023. There is currently a 50%
difference with their Chinese competitors2.
The disruption to Russian gas supplies since 2022 is behind the rising

gas prices, particularly as the EU has had to turn to liquefied natural
gas3 (LNG), which is more expensive than gas transported via pipe -
line and, above all, exposes Europe to global gas market conditions.
The growth of LNG has, in fact, intensified competition between the
major consumption regions, primarily Europe and Asia. European and
Asian prices now tend to move more in tandem (Chart 1). However, in
China, the prevalence of coal — a widely available local resource —

and a more regulated electricity price help to keep industrial prices
lower. At the same time, the United States enjoys plentiful domestic
gas, which is produced at a low cost, driven by the sharp rise in shale
oil and gas production over the past decade. By contrast, since 2022,
Europe has been more exposed to the cost of imported gas (90% of its
total consumption, according to Eurostat), while the United States and
China have enjoyed plentiful domestic resources.

Fluctuations and the level of gas prices are passed on to Eu -
ropean electricity prices via the ‘merit order’ marginal pricing
mechanism, with the wholesale price determined by the margi -
nal cost of the last (and most expensive) electricity source called
upon to meet demand.
As a result, gas plays a decisive role, with gas-fired power sta -
tions setting the price in around 40% of cases in Europe4, with si-
gnificant variations depending on national electricity mixes. It is

close to this average in Germany, below it in Spain (26%) and
France (17%), and above it in Italy (73%) 5 in 2025 6 7. Therefore,
electricity prices are still sensitive to fluctuations in gas prices,
even when electricity is not generated from gas (Chart 2). This
pass-through is particularly pronounced when demand rises
and requires the use of more expensive sources, or when supply
constraints arise (for example, during extreme heatwaves that
reduce wind-power generation or disrupt the cooling of nuclear

power plants).
1 Prices – Electricity 2026 – Analysis – IEA
2 The difference may be even larger for other manufacturers who do not receive the same support as electricity-intensive sectors.
3 Imports of Russian gas (via pipeline and LNG) accounted for around 45% of total European imports in 2021, compared with 14% in Q2 2026 (Bruegel, natural gas imports).
4 ACER, 2026, Key developments in EU electricity and gas markets, 2026 Monitoring report.
5 In Italy, in response to sharply rising gas prices in 2026, the government is currently negotiating a potential option of capping the prices of gas supplied to power stations in

order to limit the rise in the wholesale price of electricity. However, this can only be a temporary general mechanism, with the potentially major cost being borne by public funds
and/or households.
6 Eurelectric, 2026, Power Barometer.
7 EMBER, 2026, Latest energy shock reminds Europe of its risky reliance on gas.
8 Energy shock: 2026 vs. 2022 dashboard – 2nd October 2026 – Economic Studies – BNP Paribas
However, for the time being, the 2026 energy crisis is having a far

smaller effect on electricity prices than in 2022. On the one hand, the
2026 peak reached by the European TTF benchmark is still well below
that recorded in 20228.
In addition, there has been significant progress in decarbonising Eu -
ropean electricity mixes since 2022, which has helped to mitigate, on
average, the pass-through of gas prices to electricity prices.

SOME EUROPEAN COUNTRIES HAVE MANAGED TO STAY
COMPETITIVE, BUT GERMANY IS STILL THE EXCEPTION
Electricity prices partly vary due to countries’ electricity mixes
(Chart 3). Electricity-intensive manufacturers in countries where the
share of fossil energy is high face higher costs (Poland, Italy and Ire -
land) (Chart 4). Conversely, countries with a less carbon-intensive mix

(particularly France and Spain in recent times) are less directly ex -
posed to these fluctuations, with costs closer to the United States and
China (Chart 5).
The situation in Germany is paradoxical, as, despite progressively
greening its electricity mix and wholesale electricity prices (averaging
90 EUR/MWh over the 2023–2025 period) and average electricity costs
(across all industrial sectors) in line with the European average, the most
energy-intensive industries face higher costs than the European average

(and even more so compared with international competitors). This is
mainly due to grid costs and a tax burden above EU standards (Chart 6).
9 Eurostat data are used to compare prices with the rest of the EU. However, given that electricity prices are not uniform for businesses at a European level, there may be differences
between Eurostat and national sources. Jäger P ., 2025, “Cautionary tale or model for Europe? How member states should react to Germany’s new electricity subsidy”, Hertie School,

Jacques Delors Centre.
10 P . Devaux, 2026, “European Union: low carbon transition and energy sovereignty, a path fraught with obstacles”
In the first half of 2026, for the most electricity-intensive industries,
the difference compared with the European average was 9%, based on
the prices excluding taxes and levies provided by Eurostat. This gap is
mainly due to the cost of accessing the grid. If all taxes and levies are
included, the difference rises to 21% (Chart 7)9.

EUROPE’S APPROACH OF ADOPTING BOTH STRUCTURAL
PROGRAMMES AND TARGETED SUPPORT MEASURES
European programmes to support the low-carbon transition pursue
objectives relating to climate action (reducing greenhouse gas emis -
sions), sovereignty10 (increasing energy self-sufficiency) and competi -
tiveness (reducing the volatility and costs associated with fossil fuels).
The RePowerEU programme, which was launched in 2022 in response
to the energy crisis, primarily aims to reduce imports of Russian gas

and, more generally, to accelerate the low-carbon transition. Although
Europe has reduced its dependence on imports of Russian fossil fuels,
there has still not been enough progress around the energy transition
and cross-border connectivity. This is due to governance constraints

and struggles in implementing the programme in practice within each
Member State11.
State aid is still the main vehicle for this. In response to the 2021–
2022 energy crisis, the European Union temporarily relaxed the rules
on this type of aid in order to support businesses facing rising energy
prices. Any intervention of this kind, provided that it is selective and

could affect trade within the single market, must be authorised in ad -
vance by the European Commission in principle. Since June 2025, the
Clean Industrial Deal State Aid Framework (CISAF) has provided a more
sustainable framework for these kinds of interventions. In particular,
this framework, which runs until the end of 2030, authorises Member
States to grant temporary aid to electricity-intensive businesses to off-
set the cost of electricity. The aid must be targeted and come with

decarbonisation commitments12. Against the backdrop of the crisis in
the Middle East, the temporary METSAF scheme, adopted in April 2026,
helped (in relation to measures falling under the CISAF) to increase the
proportion of additional costs offset by governments from 50% to 70%,
thereby offering additional support to the most at-risk sectors.
GERMANY IS STILL SUFFERING FROM INSUFFICIENT IN-
VESTMENT IN ITS ELECTRICITY NETWORKS, WHICH THE
AID SCHEMES ARE ATTEMPTING TO OFFSET

The issue of electricity costs is a particularly hot issue in Germany,
given the importance of industry to the economy (around 20% of va -
lue added, one of the highest levels in Europe) and its exposure to
international competition. Furthermore, the delay in modernising
electricity infrastructure (only 22.6% of the high-voltage lines planned
for 2025 had actually been completed by that date) is causing bottle -
necks between the production areas in the north and the consump -

11 European Court of Auditors, 2026, “Implementation of the REPowerEU plan must be stepped up”.
12 It applies to the most electricity-intensive activities, which are seeing their competitiveness threatened by international competition, and follows the 4x50 principle: 50% of
electricity consumption is eligible for a subsidy; Of the eligible proportion, the subsidy may not exceed 50% of the wholesale price;
The final wholesale price paid by the company must not be less than €50/MWh;

50% of the aid received must be reinvested in decarbonisation (renewable energy generation, storage and improvements in energy efficiency); Around 90 sectors are covered by this
reform, accounting for approximately 2,000 companies, including in the chemical, steel and paper industries. The scheme, which is strictly regulated, will run for just three years
(from 1st January 2026 to the end of 2028) at an estimated total cost of between EUR 3 and 5 bn, financed by the special Climate and Transformation Fund.

13 Bundesnetzagentur Monitoring Report (2015) and Bundesnetzagentur Monitoring Report (2025). These costs mainly cover the costs of physical infrastructure; operational,
regulatory and compliance costs; and costs associated with using the transmission and distribution network.
14 The Industriestrompreis (industrial electricity price) is the public subsidy mechanism aiming to reduce the price of electricity for some industries.
15 The indirect carbon cost compensation mechanism is a government support measure for electricity-intensive industries, which aims to offset the cost of carbon-emission

allowances for energy producers.
tion centres in the south. As a result, the costs of accessing services
have increased fivefold over 10 years (rising from EUR 1 bn in 2014
to EUR 5.2 bn in 2024 13). At the same time, as we have already seen,
taxation and grid charges are hitting industry hard, with levies on large
consumers far exceeding the European average despite some support
mechanisms being put in place for the most electricity-intensive com-

panies (such as the Bandlastprivileg measure to reduce grid tariffs).
As a result, German industrial production has suffered in particular
due to rising energy prices in recent years, including electricity, with
electricity-intensive industries being especially affected (Chart 8).
In order to cope with these higher electricity costs than the European
average, Germany has been using European support schemes to reduce
the cost of electricity for businesses, particularly since the Merz go -

vernment took office in May 2025. The main scheme — the Industries-
trompreis14, made possible by the CISAF — has been approved by the
European Commission for the 2026–2028 period. While it is less bene-
ficial than the indirect carbon cost (ICC) offsetting included in electri -
city prices15, it has a broader scope, meaning that businesses ineligible
for the ICC can benefit from it. Other measures complement this targe-
ted support by addressing other components of the electricity bill. As a

result, the electricity tax has been set at the European minimum for
manufacturing sectors, while the gas-storage tax has been abolished.
The government is also subsidising grid fees, which should halve them.
Overall, according to our estimates based on 2024 prices, the final
price of electricity for these industries could fall by around a quarter
(to 116 EUR/MWh for industries with annual consumption of between
70 and 150 GWh, compared with a European average of 123 EUR/MWh)

(Chart 9). Germany’s advantage over Central and Eastern European
countries, where electricity costs are generally already higher, looks
set to increase.

However, even when taking all of the subsidies into account, the price
of electricity in Germany would remain higher than the levels seen in
France, Spain or Portugal (and even more so in the United States and
China).
FISCAL MEASURES: LIMITED ROOM FOR MANOEUVRE
There are a number of limitations to these European schemes. For
the country implementing them, lower electricity costs mean that

its industry becomes more competitive against industries from other
EU countries, but this does very little to address the disadvantage that
it faces compared with the United States and China. Therefore, this
measure will unlikely be enough to counteract the loss of market share
seen vis-à-vis the United States and China. In addition, the support
schemes are restrictive (conditional on investment in the low-carbon
transition) and are transitional. Finally, their cost is partly passed on

to the public purse or to consumers who do not receive this support
(in Germany, the cost of the new energy price support measures is
estimated at EUR 14.3 bn in 2026, or 0.3% of GDP16).
Some European manufacturers have proposed abolishing or reducing
the carbon tax as a solution to getting electricity prices down quickly.
In Germany, this tax accounted for 14% of the electricity price in the
first half of 2025, which is higher than the EU average (11%). However,

this proposal is already covered by the indirect carbon cost offsetting
mechanism for electricity-intensive industries exposed to carbon al -
lowance prices. In addition, while adjustments are being made — no -
tably to slow the rate at which allowances are reduced — it seems
highly unlikely that the scheme will be significantly challenged. It is
one of the cornerstones of Europe’s policy to combat climate change,
which has been effective thus far, as shown by the falling greenhouse

gas emissions from industry. More broadly, the gradual introduction of
a carbon border adjustment mechanism (CBAM) should mitigate com-
petition distortions caused by environmental practices17.
IN GERMANY AND THE REST OF EUROPE, HEAVY INVEST -
MENT IS DRIVING UP ELECTRICITY BILLS IN THE SHORT
TERM
In response to the challenge of the low-carbon transition and indus -
trial competitiveness, European governments must strike a balance
between decarbonising the electricity mix and ensuring a supply of

energy that is flexible enough (particularly from controllable sources)
to guarantee stable electricity systems. The integration of an increa -
sing proportion of intermittent renewable energy sources requires
massive investment in transmission and storage infrastructure (statio-
nary batteries). This expenditure, which places a long-term burden on
grid costs, reduces the scope for support schemes (particularly public
ones) to address competitiveness gaps. In Germany, the cost of the

transition is estimated at between EUR 122 bn, according to the go -
vernment (2.8% of GDP), and EUR 200 bn, according to the DIHK18 (4.6%
of GDP), by 2030–2035. If we take the ECB’s assumption19 that govern-
ments will cover around 17% of these investments, the annual public
16 The Industriestrompreis is estimated at around EUR 1.3 bn per year, the subsidy for network charges is projected to amount to EUR 6.5 bn in 2026, the abolition of the gas sur -

charge around EUR 3.4 bn, and the reduction in the electricity tax nearly EUR 3 bn per year. Furthermore, the ICC had cost EUR 2.3 bn in 2024 for costs incurred in 2023.
17 Jean-Luc Proutat, European Union: the carbon tax at the border in six questions, 6 October 2025.
18 German Chamber of Commerce and Industry, “Frontier economics, DIHK, 2025, New pathways for the energy transition (‘Plan B’)”.

existing gas-fired power stations) and 2 GW potentially allocated to batteries (accounting for 80% of existing large-capacity stationary batteries intended for the wholesale market).

expenditure burden of the transition in Germany would be between
0.5 and 0.8 percentage points of GDP by 2030. However, this budgetary
pressure comes at the same time as three other budgetary increases:
rising social expenditure linked to an ageing population; the necessary
increase to defence budgets to meet NATO’s target of 3.5% of GDP by
2029; and the rising cost of debt-servicing20.
Germany has already made significant progress in maximising its use

of renewable energy, putting it at the head of the pack in Europe. The
country generates 20% of Europe’s renewable electricity (solar and
wind) and accounts for 30% of installed storage capacity (BESS) 21, far
ahead of other European countries (ranking third globally). However,
despite this momentum, Germany’s dependence on gas has not yet
decreased. As a matter of fact, it is only partially offsetting the planned
closure of coal-fired power stations and the phasing out of nuclear

reactors. Furthermore, the intermittent nature of solar and wind power
could prolong this dependence in the short and medium term. In order
to ensure that the German electricity system is flexible, new invest -
ments in gas-fired power plants, supported by public subsidies, are
planned22.

SUBSIDIES ARE HELPING TO ADDRESS THE EMERGENCY
CAUSED BY ENERGY CRISES WITHOUT RESTORING THE
INTERNATIONAL COMPETITIVENESS OF ENERGY-INTEN-
SIVE INDUSTRIES
In response to the impact of energy crises on industrial competitive -
ness, European governments are finding themselves having to strike a
delicate balance between providing short-term support to industries

exposed to increased international competition and continuing — if
not, accelerating — energy transition policies which will result in hi -
gher costs for electricity-network users for several years to come.
Beyond direct economic support measures, which are difficult to sus -
tain long term, four priority areas are emerging:
1/ Demonstrating flexibility in implementing European directives in
order to factor in national specificities and the differing timeframes

between energy crises (which can lead to irreversible industrial conse-
quences) and reforms (which have a longer-term horizon). The relaxa-
tion of the European carbon tax mechanism 23 appears to be a step
towards this.
2/ Continuing efforts to decarbonise the electricity mix, which are still
the main vehicle for sustainably reducing electricity prices for Euro -
pean industries in the medium and long term.
3/ Optimising the use of electricity networks on the demand side, in

particular through smart grids, a flagship element of the Commission’s
COM (2026) 600 reform presented last July24.
4/ Coordinating European electricity networks in order to harness the
complementarities between national systems.
Article completed on 19 October 2026

光寶科 2301 TT (電子-零組件) 
高壓放量時程仍待觀察 
投資與評價 
1. 3Q26 受惠 AI 伺服器電源及 Cloud 需求成長 ， 營收季增8.9%， 毛利率25.9%，
EPS 3.27 元創單季新高。預估 4Q26 Cloud 業務持續成長，惟 PC 及消費性
電子需求疲弱，EPS 2.96 元。 
2. 2027 年成長動能來自 ASIC 8.5kW PSU/BBU、NVIDIA 110kW Power Shelf
及新客戶導入，惟記憶體缺料、資料中心建置遞延及800VDC 放量時程延後，
限制獲利成長，預估 2026/27 年 EPS 10.84/11.49 元。 
3. 目前股價為 240.5 元(約 20.9x FY27E 修正後 EPS)，於近半年本益比區間
12~26 倍中間偏上位置 。 考量高毛利率產品的放量時間仍須等待 ， 將2027 年
目標本益比由 22 倍下調至 21 倍 ， 目標價從先前目標價278 元(22x FY27E 修
正前 EPS)下調至 242 元(21x FY27E 修正後 EPS)。

重點更新 
3Q26 AI 電源占比持續提升 
3Q26 營收 574.03 億元（+8.9% QoQ、+27.9% YoY） ，低於原估3.1%，受惠 AI
伺服器電源及 Cloud 業務持續成長，其中 Cloud/AIoT 營收 337.53 億元（+57% 
YoY） ，占整體營收59%，惟 PC 及消費性電子需求相對疲弱。毛利 148.67 億元
（+3.6% QoQ） ，毛利率25.9%（-1.3ppts QoQ） ，高於原估1.9ppts，主要受惠
高附加價值 AI 電源比重提升及營運效率改善 ； 營業費用及其他營業損益淨額62.11
億元（+1.1% QoQ） ，占營收10.8%，營業利益 86.55 億元（+5.5% QoQ） ，營益
率 15.1%（-0.5ppts QoQ） ，高於原估3.1ppts。業外收益 11.42 億元，稅後淨利
74.29 億元，EPS 3.27 元創單季新高。整體而言，光寶在機種的晶片短缺供應影響
較小，支撐獲利持續成長。

2027 年 800VDC 放量部分遞延壓抑獲利 
預估 2027 年營收 2,529.25 億元（+19.8% YoY） ，較原估下修5.0%，成長主要來
自 CSP ASIC 平台 8.5kW PSU/BBU、NVIDIA Vera Rubin 18.3kW PSU與 110kW 
Power Shelf 持續出貨，以及新客戶與低軌衛星電源需求增加。惟記憶體缺料、客
戶資料中心建置遞延，加上 800VDC Power Rack 仍處於送樣驗證及初期導入階
段，較大規模貢獻可能延至 2028 年。預估毛利率 25.4%（+0.1ppts YoY） ，營業
費用 334.91 億元（+34.7% YoY） ，營業利益307.86 億元（+7.6% YoY） ，歸母稅
後淨利 266.08 億元 ，EPS 11.49 元 。 光寶已由單一PSU 延伸至 BBU、Power Shelf
及 Power Rack 系統整合，但高毛利新產品放量時程仍須觀察。

報告內容 
3Q26 AI 電源與 Cloud 需求推升獲利創高 
3Q26 營收 574.03 億元（+8.9% QoQ、+27.9% YoY） ，低於原估3.1%，受惠 AI 伺服器電源及 Cloud 業務持續成
長，其中 Cloud/AIoT 營收 337.53 億元（+57% YoY） ，占整體營收59%，惟 PC 及消費性電子需求相對疲弱。毛
利 148.67 億元（+3.6% QoQ） ，毛利率25.9%（-1.3ppts QoQ） ，高於原估1.9ppts，主要受惠高附加價值 AI 電
源比重提升及營運效率改善；營業費用及其他營業損益淨額 62.11 億元（+1.1% QoQ） ，占營收10.8%，營業利益
86.55 億元（+5.5% QoQ） ，營益率15.1%（-0.5ppts QoQ） ，高於原估3.1ppts。業外收益 11.42 億元，稅後淨
利 74.29 億元 ，EPS 3.27 元創單季新高 。整體而言 ， 光寶在既有機種的晶片短缺供應影響較小 ， 支撐獲利持續成長 。

4Q26 Cloud 出貨維持成長，非 Cloud 需求限制獲利 
預估 4Q26 營收 576.63 億元（+0.5% QoQ） ，低於原估3.0%，雖受惠既有 CSP ASIC 平台 8.5kW PSU/BBU 持續
出貨，但預料規格較高的 18.3kW PSU、110kW Power Shelf 將因客戶步調調整而減緩，且 PC、消費性電子需求
疲弱 ， 加上客戶記憶體缺料及年底工作天數減少 ， 使整體營收成長相對有限 。 預估毛利148.52 億元 （-0.1% QoQ），
毛利率 25.8%（-0.1ppts QoQ） ， 高於原估0.9ppts； 營業費用72.08 億元 （+16.0% QoQ） ， 費用率12.5%（+1.7ppts 
QoQ） ，營業利益76.44 億元（-11.7% QoQ） ，營益率13.3%（-1.8ppts QoQ） 。業外收益10.98 億元，稅後淨利
68.59 億元，EPS 2.96 元低於前季。其中 800VDC Power Rack 於 4Q26 以樣機出貨及客戶驗證為主，尚非主要營
收來源。

### Page 3

2026 年 AI 電源與低軌衛星帶動獲利創高 
預估 2026 年營收 2,111.77 億元（+27.1% YoY） ，較原估下修1.7%，雖受惠 AI 伺服器高瓦數 PSU、BBU、Power 
Shelf 及低軌衛星電源需求成長，AI 相關營收占比可望超過 30%，其中 8.5kW PSU/BBU 及 110kW Power Shelf已
進入量產階段 ， 低軌衛星電源出貨亦呈倍數成長 。 惟PC 及消費性電子需求疲弱 ， 限制整體營收增幅 。 預估毛利534.70
億元 （+40.6% YoY） ，毛利率25.3%（+2.4ppts YoY） ，高於原估0.8ppts；營業費用 248.68 億元 （+16.7% YoY），
費用率 11.8%（-1.0ppts YoY） ，營業利益286.01 億元（+70.9% YoY） ，營益率13.5%（+3.5ppts YoY） 。業外收
益 42.77 億元，稅前淨利 328.78 億元，歸母稅後淨利 251.80 億元，EPS 10.84 元創歷史新高。

2027 年新平台持續導入，800VDC 放量遞延限制獲利 
預估 2027 年營收 2,529.25 億元 （+19.8% YoY） ， 較原估下修5.0%，雖有成長動能來自既有 CSP ASIC 平台 8.5kW 
PSU/BBU 持續放量、NVIDIA Vera Rubin相關 18.3kW PSU 與 110kW Power Shelf出貨增加，以及新客戶導入與
低軌衛星電源規格升級。但 800VDC Power Rack 於 2027 年仍以客戶驗證及新平台初期出貨為主，較大規模貢獻
可能遞延至 2028 年，且高壓晶片行業的市場供給仍然緊缺，加上客戶端部分長短料與資料中心建置進度限制，影
響營收成長。預估毛利 642.77 億元（+20.2% YoY） ，毛利率25.4%（+0.1ppts YoY） ，低於原估0.1ppts；營業費
用 334.91 億元（+34.7% YoY） ，費用率13.2%（+1.5ppts YoY） ，營業利益307.86 億元（+7.6% YoY） ，營益率
12.2%（-1.4ppts YoY） 。業外收益25.51 億元，歸母稅後淨利 266.08 億元，EPS 11.49 元持續創下歷史新高。

### Page 4

評價 
新平台持續成長，800VDC 放量時程仍待觀察 
光寶科受惠 AI 伺服器電源功率提升 、BBU 滲透率增加及 Power Shelf 出貨成長 ，ASIC 8.5kW PSU/BBU 與 NVIDIA 
110kW Power Shelf已進入量產 ， 支撐2026–2027 年成長 。 惟記憶體缺料 、 客戶資料中心建置遞延 ， 以及800VDC 
Power Rack 放量時間可能遞延，限制短期獲利成長，因此調整2026/2027 年 EPS 至 10.84/11.49 元，分別較原估
上修 7.8%／下修 9.0%。考量 AI 電源系統整合能力持續提升，但新平台量產進度仍有不確定性，目前光寶科股價
為 240.5 元(約 20.9x FY27E 修正後 EPS)，於近半年本益比區間 12~26 倍中間偏上位置。考量高毛利率產品的放量
時間仍須等待，將 2027 年目標本益比由 22 倍下調至 21 倍，目標價從先前目標價 278 元(22x FY27E 修正前 EPS)
下調至 242 元(21x FY27E 修正後 EPS)。

產業概況 
高電壓直流未來將成主軸，從 48/50 V 架構走向 ±400 V/800 V DC 的架構升級，以降低配電銅損、縮短轉換鏈，
支撐高功率 AI 機櫃。800 V DC 配電正成為新藍圖的一環。機櫃級功率已把 500 kW+ 機櫃、乃至 MW-class 機
櫃，對 PSU 密度、效率、散熱與安全冗餘標準更高。 除了既有 ORV3 生態，OCP 官方也釋出 High Power Rack 
v3（HPRv3）50 V 側邊供電設計，凸顯在相容 48/50 V 負載同時，為高功率機櫃優化匯流排與供電路徑。 Open 
Compute Project Power shelf 功率級距包括了 72–100 kW 等級 power shelf、2U 72 kW BBU，效率達到 97.5–
~98%。 配電層次上移：從 PSU 單體到 rack/row-level 電源子系統（含 power shelf、bus-bar、直流配電、儲
能/BBU、監控韌體一體化） ，以模組化、可熱抽換、快速部署滿足 AI 機房擴張。 電源×冷卻協同設計：高密度液
冷標準化與 800 V DC 配電並行，被視為 AI 資料中心的兩大支柱。 
       圖 3. 光寶機架電源發展藍圖

資料來源: 光寶科, 國泰證期研究部整理 
公司簡介 
光寶科技股份有限公司成立於 1990 年，2024 年產品分為三大業務，光電部門佔 20%、消費電子佔 43%、雲端及
物聯網部門佔 37%。過去在 2022 年集團合併旗下四家公司，光寶電子、旭麗公司、源興科技、致福公司，以源興
科技為存續公司，並更名為光寶科技迄今。公司為 LED、電源供應器、機殼、影像產品生產之領導廠商。在光電產
品-LED 光電半導體同業包括了歐司朗 、 日亞化學 、 億光電子 。 雲端 、 資訊與消費性電子產品-電源供應器 台達電子 、
康舒科技、群光電等。三大事業部如下:

### Page 5

圖 4. 光寶科產品組合

資料來源: 光寶科，國泰證期研究部整理

光電半導體: LED 包含可見光和不可見光兩大類 LED 元件產品，此外亦包含戶外照明模組。應用於各資訊產品、消
費性電子、車用照明及 UV 殺菌等領域。在高效率 LED 光源產品方面，汽車照明客戶涵蓋歐洲、美洲與日本等一線
電動車車廠。不可見光 LED 產品涵蓋光耦合器、紅外線感測元件、環境光源感應器等。光耦合器目前為全球光耦合
器出貨量第一。紅外線感測裝置則應用於生物辨識及檢測、AR/VR、移動機器人等相關領域。 
車用電子: 擁有超過 40 年之汽車電子產品開發生產經驗，在車燈模組方面，生產各項車內外 LED 照明模組產品，
從車內照明如氛圍燈，到車外照明包含智慧頭燈控制模組、前後霧燈模組、轉向燈車燈模組等已運用於全球知名汽
車大廠產品中。車用鏡頭模組、感測元件等則廣泛應用於汽車智慧化裝置中，ADAS 輔助駕駛系統、DMS 駕駛監測
系統等。 
伺服器與電源應用: 於 96 年與力信興業合併，生產之電源供應器有超過三成為高階電源管理系統，應用於網通與伺
服器等企業雲端運算高階產品。其電源已開發 750W Desktop 電源供應器, 採用第三代半導體元件與搭配特殊散熱
結構，達成高效鈦金高功率密度(26.9 W/in³)與無風扇設計，1,800W 全數位控制工作站電源供應器，其功率密度達
37 W/in³，最新伺服器用 PSU 已達 100 W/in³ 。雲端運算電源方面，除提供伺服器電源供應器，亦提供大型資料
中心客戶高階伺服器電源管理系統，電源管理系統除了整合電源與電池儲能供電系統外，亦結合液冷式散熱解決方
案，同時整合光寶軟體、韌體及硬體開發能力。伺服器電源方面推出符合 OCP 標準的 10kW, 48V 輸出，高效率
(>97.5%)資料數據中心專用的伺服器電源 ， 適用於最新21 吋開放式機架系統 。 更進一步開發整機櫃500kW 系統 ，
滿足下一代 AI 伺服器高功耗能源需求。在液冷解決方案和 AI 應用方面，已完成：(1)換熱器：600kW 液對液列間
型 CDU 和 60kW 液對氣邊櫃型熱交換器兩大產品，並被列入 Nvidia RVL (2)水歧管：標準型 27OU / 48RU 水歧管
已列入 Nvidia RVL (3)機櫃：21"和 19" (MGX) 款分別列入 Nvidia NVL，也為雲端服務供應商和企業客戶提供定製
款機櫃。

## Document 568: 主动科技两轮融资约8亿元，侵入式脑机接口竞逐临床转化

Source PDF: all report pdf/主动科技两轮融资约8亿元，侵入式脑机接口竞逐临床转化.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-04
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

11 ⽉ 4 ⽇，主动科技宣布完成 4.64 亿元天使 + 轮融资，由⾼榕创投、国投先导、上海科创投
联合领投。公司称本轮融资规模创国内脑机接⼝领域天使 + 轮融资纪录。  ⽬前，国内侵⼊
式脑机接⼝企业正处于不同产品转化阶段。
摘要：
11 ⽉ 4 ⽇，上海主动科技有限公司（以下简称：主动科技）宣布完成 4.64 亿元天使 + 轮融资，由
⾼榕创投、国投先导、上海科创投联合领投。
跟投⽅包括康君资本、中⽹投、国泰海通、浦东创投、上海科创基⾦、⼴药资本等，⽼股东道
彤资本、华⼤松⽲、英诺资本继续追加投资。本轮融资将⽤于侵⼊式脑机接⼝研发、临床转化
与产业化建设。
此前，公司于 8 ⽉ 3 ⽇披露 3.3 亿元天使轮融资，两轮披露时间相隔约三个⽉，合计约 8 亿元。
主动科技称，本轮融资规模创国内脑机接⼝领域天使 + 轮融资纪录，天使轮与天使 + 轮合计⾦额
创该领域天使轮系列累计融资纪录。
主动科技成⽴于 2026 年 2 ⽉，由临港实验室孵化，专注侵⼊式脑机接⼝，产品覆盖柔性电极、专
⽤芯⽚、编解码算法与系统集成的全链路⾃研，布局运动、视觉、情绪三条产品线。运动脑机

接⼝产品 " 天枢 " ⾯向脑卒中、脊髓损伤、渐冻症等导致的运动与语⾔功能障碍患者。
公司核⼼团队研发的 1024 通道侵⼊式脑机接⼝系统于 2025 年 11 ⽉取得型式检验报告，型式检验
是产品进⼊临床试验前的基础安全核验。
公司 8 ⽉披露，计划以 1024 通道系统启动临床试验。截⾄本轮融资披露，公司未公布试验启动时
间、⼊组进展与研究医院。公司此前披露已建成约 2500 平⽅⽶办公及 GMP ⻋间，计划 2026 年底
前建成超 1 万平⽅⽶新总部。
国内侵⼊式脑机接⼝企业⽬前处于不同产品转化阶段。

## Document 605: 凱基投顧_2383 台光電_林祐熙_20261028

Source PDF: all report pdf/凱基投顧_2383 台光電_林祐熙_20261028.pdf

Date: 2026-10-28
Pages: 8

### Page 1

十月 28, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新  印刷電路板 ‧ 台灣

台光電 
(2383.TW /2383 TT)

高階 CCL 長約需求強勁，擴產延伸至 2030 年 
重要訊息 
公司公布 3Q26 季報，毛利率及營利率 37.7%、30.7%優於共識 35.7%、
28.9%，帶動淨利達 145 億達每股盈餘 40.5 元，優共識 15%。 
評論及分析  
3Q26 漲價效應帶動獲利優共識 15%。終端組合中基礎建設營收達佔比 75-
80%，手持裝置佔比 10-15%，車用佔比 5-10%。產品組合營收中  M7 以
上佔比 65-70%，M7 與 M8 皆為 30-35%，M9 低個位數。  
4Q26 啟動新一波漲價。 4Q26 營收成長主要來自漲價( 漲幅 5-20%，但不預
期高於 2Q26)、組合升級、少量新增產能貢獻。我們預估營收 可季增 15-
20%，毛利率有望持續季增。  
高階 CCL 需求樂觀，擴產計畫延伸至 2030 年。因高階 CCL 的長約 LTA 需
求量已超越原擴產規模 ，公司決定延伸擴產計畫。 摘要如下：(1)2 026 維持
585 萬張，而  2027- 30 產能最新規劃為 900 萬張、 1045 萬張、 1195 萬
張、1525 萬張，分別年增率 54%、16%、14%、28%；觀音廠原訂 2026
年 12 月開出 30 萬張，延後至 2H27，崑山廠有機會提前於 26 年 12 月開出
10 萬張部分產能。 (2) 2027 年底載板總產能預估 120 萬張，較目前崑山廠
30 萬張增加 90 萬張。(3)M9 2027 年佔比提升至雙位數，受惠 AI Server 及
Switch 導入；M10 26 年底有機會完成認證於 2H27 量產：公司認為 M10 相
較 PTFE 具備加工良率及成本優勢。(4)高階玻纖布及 HVLP 3/4 銅箔供應相對
緊張，已提前備料與廠商 擴產協調。(5) 除 AI Server 及 Networking 外，管
理層看好機器人與低軌衛星的長期成長。  
投資建議  
在 3Q26 財報優預期下，我們上調 2027 年獲利 36%，目標價上調至 8310
元相當於 2027 年每股盈餘 32 倍本益比( 原 40 倍本益比，下調 用以反應
2H27 原物料如玻纖布漲勢趨緩、 M7 以上供應者增加、 PTFE 競爭等風險)。 
投資風險  
客戶需求不如預期；競爭對手進度超前。

台灣 台光電

十月 28, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R   5

ESG 圖表定義 
項目 定義 說明 
能源使用 
直接與間接能源消耗總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司運營範圍內消耗之能源總量 
- 能源使用總量 = 直接能源消耗總量 + 間接能源消耗量 
- 總能源使用量包括購買的能源、生產的能源 
- 就公用事業而言，輸電/電網損耗為其業務活動之一部分，被視為總能源消耗，數據不包括
為滿足能源使用而生產的電力（公用事業為出售而生產） 
- 就公用事業而言，用於能源生產的煤、天然氣或核能等原料不屬於「總能源使用」項下

購買之再生能源 
Primary再生能源購買總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司各種來源之能源消耗量與購買的能源中屬於自然界可再生者（太陽能、風能、水能、
生物質能、地熱能）之量  
- 如無證據顯示再生能源由公司生產，則所報告的能源數據視為購買的再生能源

再生能源使用率 再生能源占總能源消耗量之比例  
CO2 約當排放量 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量(公噸) 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化
合物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 (NF3)

CO2 約當排放量營收比 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量（公噸）銷售（百萬元）比 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化
合物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 (NF3)

廢棄物總量 
廢棄物總量(公噸) 
- 總廢棄物 = 無害廢棄物 + 有害廢棄物 
- 僅考慮固體廢棄物，但如液體廢棄物以公噸為單位呈報，則會將其加入求得包含液體廢棄
物之總量 
- 對於採礦、石油與天然氣等行業，tailings、廢石、煤、飛灰等廢棄物亦考慮在內

廢棄物回收率 
公司呈報的廢棄物回收率 
- 廢棄物回收率 = 廢棄物回收量/總廢棄物*100 
- 廢棄物轉化為能源或經由廢棄物焚燒產生能源視為廢棄物回收 
- 經由堆肥回收的廢棄物視為回收之廢棄物

總取水量 
總取水量(立方公尺) 
- 由呈報組織直接或經水公司等中介機構從任何水源抽取的總水量 
- 水井、城鎮/公用事業/市政用水、河水、地表水等不同之水源均予以考慮

環保支出 
環保支出總金額 
- 所有用於環境保護，或防止、減少、控制環境因素、影響、危害的投資與支出，亦包括處
置、處理、衛生、清理支出

員工流動率 
員工流動率 
- 包括任何原因（自願或非自願）離開公司的員工，如辭職、退休、自然離職/死亡、醫療失
能、冗員、裁員、重組、解僱、裁減或定期合約到期 
- 員工流動率 =（離開之員工/平均員工人數）*100 
- 平均員工人數 =（本年末員工人數 + 去年末員工人數）/2 
- 本財年末員工數 =上個財年末員工數 + 新員工數 - 離開之員工數

女性經理 
女性經理百分比 
- 女性經理占公司經理的百分比 
- 如有不同階層之百分比，如最高階、高階、中階、初階，則會考慮中階女性經理之百分比 
- 女性經理百分比 = 女性經理人數/經理總數*100

女性員工 
女性員工百分比 
- 女性員工占公司員工總數的百分比 
- 女性員工百分比 = 女性人數/員工總數*100

教育訓練總時數 
所有員工教育訓練總時數 
- 僅考慮員工教育訓練時數 
- 包括一般員工所有類型的教育訓練（如健康與安全、環境、急難事件救援、技能與職業發
展） 
- 如果數據以天為單位，則乘以 8，係假設 1 天 =  8 小時工作

每位員工教育訓練時數 平均每年每位員工總教育訓練時數  
資料來源：Refinitiv；凱基

## Document 660: 大奇迹日！A股午后爆发：创业板大涨6%，科创50暴涨超9%，半导体20CM涨停潮刷屏，恒指、恒科指齐涨超1%

Source PDF: all report pdf/大奇迹日！A股午后爆发：创业板大涨6%，科创50暴涨超9%，半导体20CM涨停潮刷屏，恒指、恒科指齐涨超1%.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-04
Pages: 12
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

盘⾯上，个股呈现普涨态势，沪深京三市超 4600 股飘红，今⽇成交突破 2 万亿。沪深两市
成交额 2.04 万亿，较上⼀个交易⽇放量超 3300 亿。板块⽅⾯，半导体、通信设备、贵⾦
属、电⼦元器件、⽣物科技等板块集体爆发。⽩酒、⻝品饮料、保险、银⾏等板块逆势下
跌。
摘要：
A 股双创午后爆发，科创 50 指数⼀度狂飙超 10% ，半导体全产业链掀起总攻，分析认为，这轮爆
发的驱动是四条硬信息在同⼀时间窗⼝叠加：三季报业绩集中兑现（ 14 家设备公司前三季净利
合计 176.6 亿元、同⽐增⻓ 59.5% ）、 SEMI 给出设备销售额连续五年增⻓的景⽓锚、利基型
DRAM 供给紧张延续、以及美国中期选举结果午间落地消解了压制市场两周的政策不确定性。
11 ⽉ 4 ⽇， A 股午后直线拉升，三⼤股指集体⼤涨，沪指涨 2% ，创业板暴涨超 6% 。科创综指和
科创 50 均飙涨超 9% 。半导体全产业链狂飙， 20cm 涨停潮刷屏。算⼒硬件、存储芯⽚集体拉
升，中际旭创 306 亿成交，为两市成交额之最，存储芯⽚⻰头⻓鑫科技⼤涨 13.63% 。贵⾦属板

块午后爆发，得益于国际⾦价重回 4000 美元关⼝。
港股午后亦拉升，恒指、恒科指盘初双双上涨，恒指涨超 1% ，恒科指也涨了超 1% ，盘中⼀度跌
近 2% ，权重科⽹股反弹。芯⽚、光模块等科技股反弹，海光芯正狂涨 26% 。⽣物科技板块爆
发，恒⽣⽣物科技指数涨超 8% 。债市⽅⾯，国债期货多数上涨。商品⽅⾯，国内商品期货多数
下跌。
A 股：截⾄收盘，沪指涨 2.16% ，深成指涨 4.22% ，创业板指涨 6.40% 。
盘⾯上，个股呈现普涨态势，沪深京三市超 4600 股飘红，今⽇成交突破 2 万亿。沪深两市成交额
2.04 万亿，较上⼀个交易⽇放量超 3300 亿。板块⽅⾯，半导体、通信设备、贵⾦属、电⼦元器
件、⽣物科技等板块集体爆发。⽩酒、⻝品饮料、保险、银⾏等板块逆势下跌。

### Page 2

港股：截⾄收盘，恒指涨 1.67% ，录得四连阳，恒科指涨 1.77% 。
盘⾯上，热⻔板块普涨，半导体、医药、科技硬件板块涨幅居前，华虹宏⼒涨近 16% 领涨蓝筹；
药明⽣物、药明康德、百济神州涨超 10% 。⻩⾦股爆发，灵宝⻩⾦等多股涨超 10% 。

### Page 3

债市：国债期货多数⾛⾼，截⾄收盘， 30 年期主⼒合约持平，此前⼀度涨超 0.3% ，午后持续回
落， 10 年期主⼒合约涨 0.10% ， 5 年期主⼒合约涨 0.05% ， 2 年期主⼒合约涨 0.02% 。
商品：国内商品期货多数下跌，截⾄收盘，能源品跌幅居前，低硫燃料油跌 6.40% ；新能源材料
多数下跌，碳酸锂跌 3.91% ；化⼯品多数下跌，苯⼄烯跌 2.96% ；油脂油料多数下跌，棕榈油跌
2.91% ；基本⾦属多数下跌，锡跌 2.70% ；⾮⾦属建材涨跌参半， PVC 跌 2.58% ；农副产品多数
下跌，⽣猪跌 1.19% ；航运期货全部下跌，集运指数 ( 欧线 ) 跌 0.12% ；贵⾦属涨幅居前，钯涨
4.10% ；⿊⾊系涨跌参半，铁矿⽯涨 1.56% 。

### Page 4

半导体设备：业绩、订单、景⽓预测三重共振， 20CM 涨停潮刷屏
半导体设备是当⽇涨幅最⾼、涨停密度最⾼的细分。拓荆科技、江丰电⼦、中科⻜测、华峰测
控、芯源微、京仪装备、富创精密、正帆科技、托伦斯、强⼀股份、⾼凯技术集体 20cm 封板；
盛美上海涨 19.82% 逼近涨停。北⽅华创涨停，⻓川科技涨 18.01% 、华海清科涨 17.97% 、兴福
电⼦涨 17.25% 、国科微涨 17.22% 、华虹宏⼒涨 17.25% 。制造端中微公司⼤涨 16.62% ，君正股
份涨 17.80% ，中芯国际涨 9.00% 。

### Page 5

逻辑链的第⼀环是三季报的业绩兑现。据市场统计， 14 家半导体设备公司前三季度营收合计
759.38 亿元、同⽐增⻓ 19.3% ，归⺟净利润 176.60 亿元、同⽐增⻓ 59.5%—— 利润增速是收⼊增
速的三倍，规模效应开始显性化。

### Page 6

第⼆环是全球景⽓锚的重估。SEMI 预测 2026 年全球半导体设备销售额达 1659 亿美元、同⽐增⻓
23.2% ， 2028 年有望升⾄ 2295 亿美元、连续五年增⻓，其中 DRAM 设备 2026 年预计增⻓
39.0% 、 NAND 设备增⻓ 30.7% ，存储扩产成为最陡峭的增⻓曲线。中信建投指出全球景⽓周期
持续确认，设备销售额预计连续五年增⻓⾄ 2028 年。存储与先进逻辑扩产共同拉动设备需求，
AI 推升⼯艺复杂度，设备⼚商同时吃到了 " 产能增加 " 与 " 单机价值量提升 " 两层红利。
第三环是存储供给紧张从需求侧加固逻辑。兆易创新在投资者交流中表⽰，利基型 DRAM 供需态
势虽有缓和但仍短缺，整体产能供给有限，预计价格仍将在⾼位维持⼀段时间。供给紧张意味
着晶圆⼚没有理由放缓扩产，设备订单的可⻅度进⼀步延⻓。
第四环是个股层⾯的催化点缀。北⽅华创 10 ⽉底完成对诺合键维的全资收购，其纳⽶级对准精
度的混合键合设备直击 HBM 、 CPO 及 3D 异构集成的核⼼需求，补上先进封装最紧缺的⼀环 ——

国产设备从成熟制程向先进封装延伸，平台化叙事兑现。市场传闻的外部扰动亦被证伪：中际
旭创当⽇明确澄清，某外资券商下调其评级和⽬标市值的消息不属实。
东吴证券认为，半导体设备前道规模效应逐步显现， AI 算⼒建设带动先进逻辑、先进存储及先进
封装设备需求，国产设备在客⼾验证、量产导⼊和份额提升⽅⾯仍有较⼤空间，看好国产设备
商陆续兑现业绩。腾讯系投研观点进⼀步指出，随着三季报落地，市场定价锚正由 2026 年切向
2027 年，跨年度订单储备与盈利增⻓的可⻅度成为核⼼线索。
存储与⻓鑫科技：上调回购⾄ 50 亿，昨⽇创新低的 DRAM ⻰头今⽇暴涨
13.63%
存储是本轮半导体的第⼆战场。存储芯⽚概念上涨 9.51% ，⾼带宽内存概念上涨 11.53% ，中芯
概念、⼤基⾦持股指数均涨超 10% 。个股层⾯，⻓鑫科技⼤涨 13.63% ，全天成交 209.97 亿元，
居全市场第三位；普冉股份涨逾 15% ，兆易创新涨 8.48% ，澜起科技涨 11.87% ，雅克科技涨
停，先导基电、有研新材涨停。

### Page 7

⻓鑫科技是当⽇存储线最有戏剧性的⼀笔。这家 7 ⽉ 27 ⽇才登陆科创板的 DRAM 设计制造⼀体化
⻰头，前三季度实现营收 2690.24 亿元、同⽐增⻓ 738.51% ，归⺟净利润 1320.40 亿元；第三季
度营收 1187.14 亿元、同⽐增⻓ 613.18% ，归⺟净利润 544.35 亿元。
但三季报披露后股价连续两⽇下跌， 11 ⽉ 3 ⽇收跌 7% ，创下上市以来收盘新低 —— 净利润环⽐
增速放缓（ Q3 环⽐仅 +3.01% ）被市场解读为存储涨价周期⻅顶的信号，管理层随后在投资者活
动中解释，这主要受⼦公司⻓鑫新桥、⻓鑫集电权益⽐例较低的会计结构影响，两家⼦公司 Q3
营收和利润环⽐增速实际都超过 50% 。
转折发⽣在今⽇盘前。董事⻓朱⼀明提议将回购⾦额由 "10 亿⾄ 20 亿元 " ⼤幅上调⾄ "25 亿⾄ 50 亿
元 " ，创下科创板史上最⾼单次回购⾦额纪录。管理层同场给出经营指引：⽬前公司⻓协⼯作进
展顺利，已与多家头部客⼾协商⻓期供货协议； DDR5 等主⼒产品明显供不应求，对企业级业务

持续增⻓保持乐观，预计第四季度⽑利率继续保持稳定。叠加北⽅华创收购诺合键维切⼊ HBM
混合键合、兆易创新确认利基 DRAM 短缺延续，存储 " 涨价周期未完 " 的叙事⼀夜之间重新闭合，
昨⽇被砸出的⻩⾦坑当天被资⾦填平。
算⼒硬件⼤反弹：中际旭创 306 亿成交辟谣砍单， AMD 财报与云⼚商资本
开⽀双保险
昨⽇被 Marvell 收购 Lumentum 传闻砸出深坑的算⼒硬件，今⽇全线修复。 CPO 概念上涨
7.98% 、光通信模块上涨 7.64% 、 PCB 上涨 6.91% 。
个股上，中际旭创涨 11.07% ，全天成交 306.69 亿元、为两市成交额之最；新易盛涨 13.35% ，成
交 210.25 亿元、居第⼆位；天孚通信涨停，胜宏科技涨 10.21% 、⽣益科技涨 9.49% 、⼯业富联
涨 7.87% 、⽴讯精密涨 8.93% 、东⼭精密涨 7.04% ； PCB 端沪电股份涨停，深南电路涨停，兴森
科技、华正新材、⼴合科技涨停，⾦安国纪⾛出 6 天 3 板；沐曦股份 -U 涨 16.19% ，寒武纪涨

10.38% 。

### Page 8

中际旭创表⽰：绝⼤部分客⼾未下修需求或订单，仅个别客⼾做产品结构调整，部分客⼾ 1.6T
需求较此前还有所增加；即使考虑个别客⼾结构性调整， 2027 年⾏业整体需求仍在原预测范围
内，头部客⼾需求依然强劲；公司 2027 年全年订单已基本落地，已有重点客⼾给出 2028 年强劲
需求指引；当前客⼾拿货节奏正常，光模块供给偏紧、⽆异常库存。市场昨⽇交易的 " 砍单 + 评级
下调 " 两条利空，⼀条被公司否认、⼀条被公司指为虚假消息。
第⼆重保险来⾃海外财报的景⽓验证。美东时间周⼆盘后， AMD 公布 2026 财年第三季度业绩：
营收同⽐增⻓ 45% 创历史新⾼，数据中⼼营收 85.5 亿美元、同⽐增⻓ 97% ，业绩和指引均超预
期，公司预计 2027 年服务器收⼊将同⽐翻倍、数据中⼼ AI 业务增速快于其 90 天前的预测。
华泰证券统计，北美四⼤云⼚商三季度合计资本开⽀同⽐增⻓ 108.02% ⾄ 2005.42 亿美元，并对
2026—2027 年资本开⽀维持积极指引 —— 海外 CSP 资本开⽀⾼增叠加乐观指引，持续提振市场

对算⼒需求的信⼼，并可能带动国内互联⽹⼚商加⼤投⼊。

### Page 9

贵⾦属：现货⾦重回 4000 美元，中期选举 + 央⾏购⾦双催化， 13 股集体涨
停
贵⾦属是当⽇涨停数量仅次于半导体的⽅向，且罕⻅地以万亿级⼤盘股集体涨停的形态出现。
⼭东⻩⾦涨停，⾚峰⻩⾦涨停，⼭⾦国际、招⾦⻩⾦、四川⻩⾦、西部⻩⾦、湖南⻩⾦、湖南
⽩银、盛达资源、兴业银锡、⽩银有⾊集体封板，晓程科技 20cm 涨停，中⾦⻩⾦涨 9.39% 。
有⾊权重端，紫⾦矿业涨 6.56% ，中⾦⻩⾦涨 9.39% ；⼩⾦属同步⾛强，云南锗业涨停，中钨⾼
新涨停收报 39.23 元，东⽅钽业涨停。
第⼀重催化是美国中期选举结果落地。美国选举数据分析机构 DDHQ 预测，⺠主党将获得国会参
议院 100 席中的 51 席，此前已预测其夺回众议院 —— ⺠主党重新掌控国会两院。市场解读为 " 分
权制衡 " 格局确⽴，特朗普政府扩⼤⾚字的税收与⽀出计划将受国会掣肘，美国国债全线⾛⾼、
美债收益率曲线整体下⾏，美元指数同步⾛弱，以美元计价的贵⾦属获得直接提振。
特朗普本⼈已向⺠主党表达祝贺，并称拥有完全的否决权。贵⾦属期货同步⼤涨： COMEX ⻩⾦

盘中涨 2% 重返 4000 美元上⽅、⼀度报 4019.1 美元 / 盎司，现货⽩银同步拉涨、盘中报 51.69 美元 /
盎司、涨逾 3.8% ；内盘沪⾦主⼒涨 2.31% 报 865.64 元 / 克，沪银主⼒涨 3.33% 报 12413 元 / 千克。
第⼆重催化是央⾏购⾦数据的持续强化。世界⻩⾦协会数据显⽰， 2026 年三季度全球央⾏及其
他官⽅机构净购⾦ 321 吨、同⽐增⻓ 42% ，为 2024 年四季度以来单季新⾼，年初⾄今累计购⾦
675 吨；同⽇发布的中国⻩⾦需求趋势报告显⽰，三季度中国⻩⾦总需求 192 吨、同⽐增⻓
12% 、环⽐增⻓ 23% 。中国央⾏已连续 23 个⽉增持⻩⾦，储备多元化与美元信⽤重估构成⾦价
中⻓期⽀撑。
第三重催化来⾃地缘与油价的间接助⼒。美伊对话预期升温，伊朗⽅⾯表⽰若美⽅落实相关诉
求、谈判最快可在两天内启动，油价⼤幅回落（ WTI 和布伦特盘中跌幅⼀度扩⼤⾄约 6% ），通胀
与利率压⼒缓和降低了持有⽆息资产的机会成本。
菲利普诺⽡分析师 Priyanka Sachdeva 提⽰，中期选举结果对⾦价是 " 反转推⼒还是新噪⾳ " 仍需

观察，美债收益率与美联储政策预期仍是限制因素 ——CME 数据显⽰市场对 12 ⽉加息 25 个基点
的概率定价约 79% 。

### Page 10

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
创新药与 CXO ：药明康德 810 亿成交涨停，公募加仓数据与 " 史上最佳三季
报 " 对⻬
创新药与 CXO 是午后承接科技溢出资⾦最快的⽅向。医疗研发外包⾏业上涨 8.23% ，个股上，
药明康德午后封死涨停；凯莱英涨停，昭衍新药涨停，桂林三⾦⾛出 5 天 4 板（中药），众⽣药业
涨停，益诺思涨超 15% ，皓元医药、荣昌⽣物、康⻰化成、美迪西涨超 10% 。
业绩端是 " 史上最佳三季报 " 的直接定价。药明康德前三季度实现营收 455.37 亿元、同⽐增⻓
38.59%—— 已超去年全年，归⺟净利润 193.98 亿元、同⽐增⻓ 60.64% ；在⼿订单 767.4 亿元、
同⽐增⻓ 36.4% ，较上季度环⽐净增约 100 亿元；公司将 2026 年全年收⼊指引由 585 亿⾄ 605 亿

元上调⾄ 610 亿⾄ 619 亿元。
凯莱英前三季度净利润增⻓逾 81% ，昭衍新药第三季度单季净利润同⽐增⻓逾 8 倍。外部订单环
境亦有佐证：国家药监局数据显⽰， 2026 年上半年我国创新药对外授权交易共 81 笔、总额约
1100 亿美元，已达 2025 年全年的⼋成；隔夜诺和诺德第三季度 Wegovy ⼝服药销售额 53.9 亿丹
⻨克朗、超出市场预期，减重药需求为 CXO 订单预期再添⼀层⽀撑。
资⾦端的验证同样有⼒。国信证券研报显⽰，三季度全市场公募基⾦⼤幅向 CXO 倾斜， CXO 持
仓占⽐达 36.9% 、环⽐提升 8.72 个百分点；兴业证券测算，三季度 A+H 医药板块主动基⾦持仓⽐
例 11.27% 、环⽐提升 4.93 个百分点，处于超配状态，核⼼仓位集中于创新药与创新药产业链。
兴业证券同时给出估值坐标： CRO 指数收⼊与归⺟净利润分别同⽐增⻓ 33.58% 和 85.87% ，⽽
其 PE-TTM 约 21.97 倍、处于过去七年 13% 的低分位 —— 业绩⾼增与估值低分位的组合，是当⽇

资⾦敢于把超跌的 CRO 从情绪冰点（前⼀交易⽇板块指数跌 3.17% ）直接买到涨停潮的底⽓。

積極擴產

3Q26 獲利優於預期 
聯亞 3Q26 營收 15.6 億元，季增 28%，年增 183%，成長主因資料中心產品出貨
量持續增加，隨營業額持續成長，毛利率進一步提高至 58.6%，營業費用約 1.43 
億元，較第二季增加約 1,400 萬元，增加項目主要為研發費用，以及隨獲利提升而
按比例提列的員工分紅費用。營業利益為 7.71 億元，營業利益率達 49.4%，EPS
為 6.12 元，優於預期。

需求佳且訂單能見度高 
矽光產品需求依然十分強勁，主要客戶目前均已開始下 6 個月以上的長期訂單，隨
公司與下游代工廠產能持續增加，預期矽光產品出貨量在接下來幾季將持續成長。
預計 2027 年第一季會有比較多產能開出，屆時產出數量將有更明顯增加。整體預
估 4Q26 營收為 18.7 億元，季增 20%，年增 191%，EPS 為 7.4 元。

與上下游簽訂長約 
聯亞與一家美國大型 Data Center 客戶簽訂為期 4 年的長期供貨合約 ， 並於8 月新
增約 30 億元資本支出，過去一年董事會通過的資本支出計畫總金額已超過 80 億
元 ， 將隨後續設備到位逐漸實施與支出。預計 2027 年產能較 2026 年增加 150%，
2028 年產能再較 2027 年增加 150%，兩年累計產出預計增加約 6 倍。主要客戶下
單期間均已超過 6 個月，在手訂單金額已超過 2026 全年預估營收。為確保原物料
穩定，亦與另一家日本基板供應商簽署 4 年長約，目前已與兩家日本及一家美國主
要基板供應商簽有長約。

上修今明兩年獲利預估，維持買進評等 
由於過去一兩年持續供不應求，已經很長一段時間沒有客戶提出議價，因此未看
到產品價格下滑的趨勢。針對原物料上漲，公司均有適當向客戶反應與調整，我
們預估 FY26、FY27 EPS 為 21.22 元、53.33 元，維持買進評等，上調目標價至
4,800 元，約為 FY27 PER 90x。

富邦不同之處 
市場預估 FY26、FY27 EPS 為 20.12 元、45.79 元。

評價與風險 
聯亞競爭對手為全新、IET-KY、IQE 等。評價部分，三五族磊晶產業處於一個中長
期成長趨勢中，尤其聯亞高速傳輸業務具相當高成長潛力與想像空間，獲利加速成
長階段將帶動評價提升。投資風險部分，由於聯亞客戶集中度較高，若發生單一客
戶大幅變化時，容易造成聯亞業績出現大幅波動情況。

富邦的股票評等標準 
評等  定義 
買進 預估未來6個月內的絕對報酬超過15%  
中立  預估未來6個月內有絕對報酬介於15%與負15%之間 
賣出 預估未來6個月內的絕對報酬高於負15% 
未評等 由於富邦目前與該公司有特定交易或沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等  
評估中 目前正在研議個股的投資評等，將於3到6個月內提供投資評等

產業評等 定義 
優於大盤 預估該產業在未來6個月內會比大盤指數表現突出 
持平 預估該產業在未來6個月內與大盤指數表現相較持平 
劣於大盤 預估該產業在未來6個月內會比大盤指數表現較差

Fubon's Rating System 
Rating Definition 
Buy Expected absolute return to be over 15% within the next 6 months  
Neutral  Expected absolute return to be level within the next 6 months  
Sell  Expected absolute return to be over negative 15% within the next 6 months 
Not Rated (NR)  Pursuant to Fubon acting in deals involving the company or there is not a sufficient fundamental basis for determining an investment rating  
Under Review Fubon is in the process of determining an investment rating and will assign a rating within the 3- to 6-month horizon

Sector Rating Definition 
Overweight Sector expected to outperform the broader market indexes within the next 6-12 months 
Market Weight  Sector expected to perform in line with the broader market averages within the next 6-12 months 
Underweight  Sector expected to underperform the broader market indexes within the next 6-12 months.

### Page 1

2026 年 10 月 30 日(金)

1/2 
慎重な一面も感じられた日銀 世界同時引き締めの影響は？ 
 経済調査部主席エコノミスト藤代宏一 
（℡：050-5474-6123）

＜総裁会見前に執筆＞ 
・日銀は大方の予想通り政策金利を1.25％で据え置いた。また基調的な物価上昇率が２％に到
達したとの認識を示した。展望レポートで示された成長率と物価見通しは下表のとおり、実
質ＧＤＰ成長率に大きな変化がないなか、消費者物価（除く生鮮食品）は2026年度が＋2.5％
（前回から不変）となった後、2027年度が＋2.6％へと0.2％pt上方修正され、2028年度は＋
2.0％（前回から不変）となった。なお、日銀は飲食品の消費税引き下げによって、2027年度
の消費者物価（除く生鮮食品）は前年比1.3％pt下押しされるとの試算を示したが、今回の見
通しには織り込んでいない。

・日経平均株価は先行き12 ヶ月72,000 円程度で推移するだろう 
・USD/JPY は先行き12 ヶ月 155 円程度で推移するだろう 
・日銀は政策金利を12 月に 1.5％とした後、27 年末までに2.25％とするだろう 
・FED はFF 金利を、年内は4.0％で据え置くだろう

### Page 2

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
2/2 
・なお、ＥＴＦの売却加速はほとんど予想されておらず驚きであったが、この１年間の株式市
場の売買代金増加などを踏まえれば、日銀による売却の影響は依然として小さい。

・注目されていた政策金利据え置きに対する反対票は、高田委員の１票のみであった。筆者
は、予てから「基調的な物価上昇率が２％に達している」という認識を示してきた田村委員
が金利据え置きに反対票を投じる可能性があるとみていたが、連続利上げの必要性は乏しい
との判断であった模様である。

・今回、筆者が注目したのは「経済・物価のリスク要因」を記述する箇所に、物価上昇率を下
落方向に動かし得る要因が加わったことである。これまでは現在進行中の利上げを正当化す
る意味もあってか、物価を押し上げる要因が中心であったが、今回は下記の記述が加わっ
た。

「世界的にインフレ圧力が高まるなか、夏場以降、米欧を含む多くの中央銀行は利上げ方向に
政策を転換しており、こうしたグローバルな金融環境の変化が、国際金融市場等を通じてわ
が国の経済・物価に及ぼす影響には留意が必要である」

日銀が利上げを開始した2024年と翌2025年において、欧米中銀の政策は緩和方向であった。
例えばＦｅｄは累積６回、1.75％ptの利下げを講じ、ＦＦ金利を5.5％から3.75％まで引き下
げた。それに対して2026年後半以降、主要中銀は同時に金融引き締め方向に動いており、世
界全体として金融市場にストレスがかかりやすい状況にある。事実、最近のＡＩ・半導体株
の調整は、金利上昇による直接・間接の影響がある。すなわち金利上昇による資金調達コス
トの増加がＡＩ投資そのものを減速させ得る経路もあれば、安全資産である国債利回りの上
昇が株式の相対的な魅力を低下させ株価を下押しするという経路もある。こうした金融環境
の引き締まりが、日本の景気や物価を過度に下押しする可能性を意識し始めたように感じら
れる。基調的な物価上昇率が２％に到達したとの認識を示した一方で、こうした記述からは
慎重な姿勢が窺えた。

美媒认为，⺠主党掌握参议院实际上阻断了特朗普向美联储理事会安插亲信的能⼒。⻓期
⽀持联储独⽴性的⺠主党议员沃伦预计将出任参议院银⾏委员会主席，特朗普未来提名联

摘要：
据新华社，多家美国主流媒体预测，美国⺠主党在 3 ⽇举⾏的中期选举中⼤获全胜，重新夺回国
会参议院和众议院掌控权。这⼀ “ 意外 ” ⼤胜意味着，特朗普政府未来两年在推动美联储⼈事布
局、施加货币政策影响⽅⾯将⾯临更⼤的国会阻⼒。
美媒认为，⺠主党重新掌控参议院、尤其是参议院银⾏委员会，实际上阻断了特朗普向美联储
理事会安插亲信的能⼒。⻓期⽀持美联储独⽴性的⺠主党参议员沃伦（ Elizabeth Warren ）预计
将出任银⾏委员会主席，特朗普未来提名美联储官员的过程可能遭遇更严格的审查，甚⾄被搁
置。这也可能让美联储及其主席沃什拥有更⼤的空间，通过加息抗击通胀。

对⼀直试图扩⼤对⽩宫货币政策影响⼒的特朗普⽽⾔，本次中选结果⽆疑是⼀次重⼤挫折。特
朗普此前多次要求美联储降息，并试图通过⼈事任命改变美联储的政策取向。⺠主党控制国会
后，特朗普不仅更难在美联储理事会中安插⽀持者，其对现任美联储官员施压的政治空间也可
能受到进⼀步限制。
不过，国会易主并不意味着美联储从此可以完全摆脱政治压⼒。特朗普⾝为总统仍掌握美联储
理事提名权，⾏政部⻔也可以通过法律诉讼、调查及其他⾏政⼿段向央⾏施压。⺠主党控制参
议院为美联储增加了⼀层制度性保护，但不⾜以从根本上消除联储独⽴性⾯临的⻛险。
参议院易主，特朗普的美联储⼈事布局遇阻
⺠主党此次胜选对美联储最直接的影响，是改变了理事提名与确认机制背后的国会权⼒结构。
根据美国制度，总统有权提名美联储理事，但提名⼈选必须经过参议院确认。参议院银⾏委员
会负责审查相关提名，并决定是否将⼈选提交参议院全体表决。
这意味着，⺠主党重新掌握参议院后，可以利⽤委员会审查和全院表决环节，对特朗普未来的
美联储⼈事安排施加实质性影响。
参议院银⾏委员会的权⼒交接尤其值得关注。⺠主党参议员沃伦预计将接替共和党参议员斯科

特（ Tim Scott ），出任该委员会主席。
沃伦⻓期批评特朗普试图将美联储置于⽩宫影响之下，同时⽀持央⾏在不受政治⼲预的情况下
独⽴决定利率。在她领导下，参议院银⾏委员会可能对特朗普提名的美联储官员采取更严格的
审查态度，尤其是那些被认为可能屈从于⾏政部⻔、缺乏货币政策独⽴性的候选⼈。
更重要的是，⺠主党可以通过控制委员会审查程序，阻⽌或拖延特朗普提名⼈选的确认。即使
未来美联储理事会出现空缺，⽩宫也未必能够顺利将⾃⼰属意的⼈选送⼊央⾏。这将削弱特朗
普通过⼈事任命改变美联储内部政策⽴场的能⼒。
理事库克去留成为特朗普与美联储博弈焦点

### Page 2

美联储理事库克（ Lisa Cook ）的去留问题，是观察⺠主党作为新多数党将如何影响美联储独⽴
性的⼀个重要案例。
特朗普此前试图以库克涉嫌房贷申请违规为由将其解职，但相关指控尚未得到证实。库克否认
存在⾜以构成解职理由的⾏为，并通过法律途径挑战特朗普的⾏动。
如果特朗普最终推动库克离职，⺠主党控制的参议院也可能拒绝确认⽩宫为该席位提名的继任
者。这意味着，即使特朗普能够推动不⽀持其政策⽴场的理事离开，也未必能够顺利将⾃⼰属
意的⼈选送⼊美联储。
从制度层⾯看，美国法律规定，美联储理事只能因正当理由（ for cause ）被总统免职，⽽不能
仅仅因为其货币政策⽴场与⽩宫不同就遭到解职。特朗普试图罢免在任美联储理事，在美国总
统与美联储关系史上也极为罕⻅。
因此，库克事件不仅涉及⼀名理事的去留，更关系到总统能否突破美联储⻓期形成的⼈事独⽴
与制度保障。
沃什也将⾯临更严厉审查，沃伦或追查其资产安排
⺠主党重新掌控参议院，不仅会影响特朗普未来的美联储⼈事任命，也可能给美联储主席沃什
带来新的政治压⼒。

随着沃伦预计出任参议院银⾏委员会主席，沃什未来在国会听证会上可能⾯临更加尖锐的质
询。
其中⼀个潜在焦点，是沃什在出任美联储主席前对其数亿美元资产的处置情况。沃伦此前⼀直
对相关资产安排的透明度提出质疑，⺠主党掌控的委员会也可能加强对此类问题的监督。
此外，⺠主党控制众议院⾦融服务委员会后，也可能加强对美联储及⾦融监管政策的监督，包
括银⾏监管改⾰、稳定币监管规则等议题。这意味着，特朗普政府及其⽀持者推动的⾦融监管
改⾰也可能遭遇更多国会阻⼒。
美联储独⽴性获制度性⽀撑，但⽩宫施压⻛险仍在
从货币政策⻆度看，⺠主党控制参议院可能为美联储提供更⼤的政策⾃主空间。
特朗普⻓期要求美联储⼤幅降息，希望通过更宽松的货币政策降低政府融资成本、刺激经济并
提振市场。但美联储官员仍需要根据通胀和就业数据决定利率，⽽不是简单服从总统的政策偏
好。
⺠主党掌控参议院后，特朗普更难通过新增理事任命改变美联储内部的政策⼒量对⽐。与此同
时，国会对美联储的⽀持也可能让官员在⾯对总统施压时拥有更强的制度性后盾。
不过，这并不意味着美联储从此可以⾼枕⽆忧。总统仍然掌握理事提名权，⾏政部⻔也可以通

过其他⾏政和法律⼿段向央⾏施压。⺠主党控制参议院能够阻⽌部分⼈事任命，却⽆法完全限
制⽩宫对现任美联储官员施加压⼒。
对于⾦融市场⽽⾔，此次选举的关键影响在于，美国货币政策制定机制可能获得更强的制度性
保护，特朗普通过⼈事任命推动美联储迅速降息的难度明显增加。

分治政府削减财政扩张预期，美债短期受提振

两院分治的直接影响在于⽴法层⾯。特朗普将保留否决权及相当⼴泛的⾏政权⼒，但⼤规模减

州层⾯对⼈⼯智能发展形成更⼤阻⼒，可能对中⻓期经济增⻓预期产⽣影响。彭博 Markets Live

⻛险提⽰及免责条款

华尔街静待再融资声明，⻓债缩量路径成焦点
选举结果之外，市场更为关注的是周三将公布的季度再融资声明。
⾃ 2024 年 1 ⽉以来，财政部⼀直维持稳定的中⻓期国债发⾏指引，承诺未来数个季度保持票据和
债券发⾏规模不变。华尔街多家⼀级交易商预计，本次声明可能调整这⼀政策框架，为 2027 年

年期国债发⾏规模；瑞银美国利率策略主管 Phoebe White 则认为，更可能出现的是政策措辞调
整⽽⾮即时改变拍卖规模，该⾏预计⾄少到明年 5 ⽉前实际发⾏量不会变化；摩根⼤通判断最为
保守，预计发⾏规模将⾄少维持不变⾄明年 8 ⽉，届后新增发⾏主要集中在 2 年⾄ 10 年期国债。

巴克莱策略师团队建议，财政部可将原有指引中 " ⾄少未来数个季度 " 的表述改为 " 未来数个季
度 " ，这⼀措辞的微调虽看似细⼩，却可为财政部调整发⾏结构保留更⼤灵活性，实质上意味着
不再对稳定发⾏作出明确承诺。
短债依赖加深，融资结构⾯临承接考验
若财政部削减⻓债发⾏，国库券将承担更⼤⽐例的融资压⼒。
截⾄ 9 ⽉，国库券在美国未偿市场化国债中的占⽐已升⾄约 22% ，超过美国财政部借款咨询委员
会（ TBAC ）所建议的⻓期平均 20% ⽔平。
期限不超过⼀年的国库券需频繁滚动续作，意味着财政部须持续获得市场承接。⽬前，美国财
政 部 每 周 国 库 券 拍 卖 规 模 已 超 过 5000 亿 美 元 ， 市 场 承 接 能 ⼒ 正 ⾯ 临 实 质 性 考 验 。 Insight
Investment 投资专家主管 April LaRusse 指出，单靠债券回购难以对市场产⽣⾜够影响，若财政
部有意压低收益率，调整国债发⾏结构才是更直接的⼯具。
此外，市场还将关注 TBAC 的讨论反馈，尤其是有关市场能否持续消化更⼤规模国库券发⾏的评

估意⻅。若维持现有发⾏规模不变，下周三场基准国债拍卖总额将达 1250 亿美元，包括 580 亿美
元 3 年期、 420 亿美元 10 年期以及 250 亿美元 30 年期国债。
多重数据叠加，债市近期波动加剧
周三的债市⾛势实际上是对此前跌势的修复。周⼆，受美国职位空缺数据强于预期提振，市场
押注美联储加息路径更为持久， 10 年期美债收益率在当⽇⼤幅攀升。投资者本周还需消化周五
将公布的⾮农就业数据，这将是判断美联储政策路径的⼜⼀关键参考。
综合来看，债市当前⾯临多重交织因素：⺠主党控制国会带来短期财政整固预期，但分治格局
能否持续约束财政扩张仍待观察；再融资声明将为国债供给路径提供更清晰指引；⽽美联储政
策⾛向与经济数据的持续博弈，仍是决定收益率中枢的核⼼变量。

### Page 3

## Document 808: 美国中选，今天市场怎么看

Source PDF: all report pdf/美国中选，今天市场怎么看.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-04
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

美国中期选举，众议院⺠主党胜局已被市场定价，真正变量是参议院 —— ⺠主党需净赢 4
席。概率最⼤的 “ ⺠主党横扫 ” 反⽽最不利于股市，美银视为可能跌超 10% 的⻛险事件。⾸个
信号看北京时间 11 ⽉ 4 ⽇早 8:30 俄亥俄州投票站关闭并陆续公布结果， 10 点德克萨斯、密
歇根、堪萨斯、爱荷华、内布拉斯加的初步计票结果也将逐渐公布。
摘要：
截⾄美东时间 11 ⽉ 3 ⽇上午，预测市场 Polymarket 上，⺠主党夺回众议院的概率为96%，夺回参
议院的概率为74%—— 后者是该市场开盘以来的最⾼值。⺠主党 " 全⾯横扫 " 合约的交易价格为
77%。
⾼盛在当天早间发出的 " 中期选举预览 " 报告中点明：
换句话说：众议院的结果市场已经 " 买⼊ " ，参议院的⾛向才是市场押注真正的交易。⽽概率最⼤
的 " ⺠主党横扫两院 " 反⽽最不利于美股股市，美银视为可能跌超 10% 的⻛险事件。
参议院的数学题：四席之差，七场硬仗
⽬前参议院格局为共和党 53 席、⺠主党 47 席。⺠主党需净赢得4 席，若仅赢 3 席，参议院将以 50-

50 平局收场 —— 届时美国副总统万斯的⼀票将维持共和党控制权。
Cook Political Report 将七场参议院竞选列为" 纯粹悬念 " （ toss-up ，关键摇摆州）：阿拉斯加、
爱荷华、缅因、密歇根、新罕布什尔、俄亥俄和德克萨斯。《纽约时报》 /Siena 最新⺠调显⽰，
⺠主党在密歇根、德克萨斯、堪萨斯、缅因和俄亥俄均领先，在阿拉斯加保持优势，仅在爱荷
华⼏乎与对⼿持平。
⺠主党拿下众议院的预期已基本被市场定价，参议院才是选举相关惊喜的主要来源。鉴于近期去
⻛险操作已使市场情绪、仓位和杠杆显著下降，更⼤的问题或许在于：选举结果能否成为⼀个出
清事件，并推动股市更⼴泛的⻛险重建。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

图表揭⽰了三个值得关注的细节：
在所有共和党现任席位中，⺠主党在⺠调中均领先 —— 但除北卡罗来纳州外，领先幅度均不
超过约 3 个百分点；
缅因、堪萨斯和爱荷华⼏乎处于 " 五五开 " 的分界线上；
市场给予⺠主党在德克萨斯和阿拉斯加的胜算，明显⾼于⺠调数字本⾝所能⽀撑的⽔平。
⺠调可能再次 “ 帮倒忙 ”
⾼盛政治经济学家 Alec Phillips 和 Abhay Duggirala 已就此发出数周警⽰。
⾼盛援引历史选举数据指出，⾃ 2002 年以来，在共和党倾向较强的州，共和党参议员候选⼈的
最终表现平均⽐选前⺠调⾼出约 3 个百分点。在竞争激烈的选举中，当候选⼈的⺠调领先幅度不
超过 2.5 个百分点时，其最终获胜率约为 60% 。⽬前，在共和党现任席位中，⺠主党领先幅度超
过这⼀ "3 点安全垫 " 的，只有北卡罗来纳州。
⾼盛交易台在当天早间的解读是：
参议院的竞争仍然激烈得多。近⼏个选举周期显⽰，⺠调误差往往低估共和党在倾向共和党州的
表现。因此， ' 分裂国会 ' 结果带来的市场反应，可能⽐当前定价所反映的更⼤。

### Page 3

⾼盛 FX 策略师 Stuart Jenkins 也在当⽇早报中提醒：
关键时间节点
其中，最值得关注的关键摇摆州是：北京时间 11 ⽉ 4 ⽇上午 8:30 陆续公布初步计票结果的俄亥俄
州、 10:00 的德克萨斯、密歇根、堪萨斯、爱荷华及内布拉斯加等州最值得关注。
11 ⽉ 3 ⽇美东时间下午 6:00 （北京时间 11 ⽉ 4⽇上午 7:00） ——印第安纳州、肯塔基州（两州
⼤部分地区）投票站关闭，⽽后陆续公布初步计票结果。
11 ⽉ 3 ⽇美东时间晚 7:00 （北京时间 11 ⽉ 4⽇上午 8:00） ——佐治亚州、南卡罗来纳州、弗吉
尼亚州、佛蒙特州、佛罗⾥达州⼤部分地区以及新罕布什尔州投票站关闭，⽽后陆续公布初
步计票结果。
11 ⽉ 3 ⽇美东时间晚 7:30 （北京时间 11 ⽉ 4⽇上午 8:30）——北卡罗来纳州、俄亥俄州、西弗
吉尼亚州投票站关闭，⽽后陆续公布初步计票结果。俄亥俄州是当晚参议院关键摇摆州中最
值得关注的州之⼀。Kalshi 预测市场当时给予⺠主党候选⼈ Sherrod Brown 约 71% 的胜选隐含

概率。如果 Brown 能够率先拿下俄亥俄州，⺠主党翻转参议院的可能性将显著增加；反之，
如果他落败，⺠主党夺回参议院的市场预期可能迅速降温。
美东时间晚 8:00 （北京时间 11 ⽉ 4⽇上午 9:00） ——缅因州、宾⼣法尼亚州、新泽西州、佛罗
⾥达州其他地区、新罕布什尔州、德克萨斯州⼤部分地区、堪萨斯州和密歇根州。
美东时间晚 9:00 （北京时间 11 ⽉ 4⽇上午 10:00）—— 这是观察参议院竞争格局的另⼀个重要
时间窗⼝。德克萨斯、密歇根、堪萨斯、爱荷华及内布拉斯加等州的投票站将陆续关闭，初
步计票结果也将逐渐公布。其中，德克萨斯州 Talarico 对阵 Paxton 的参议员选举尤其值得关
注。该场竞选的预测市场交易活跃度较⾼，⺠主党候选⼈ Talarico 当时的市场隐含胜率约为
74% 。如果⺠主党能够在德克萨斯这样的传统共和党优势州取得突破，将⼤幅改变市场对参
议院控制权的判断。
美东时间晚上 10 点 —— 内华达州、蒙⼤拿州、犹他州。
美东时间晚上 11 点 —— 加利福尼亚州、俄勒冈州、华盛顿州、爱达荷州。

美东时间 4 ⽇午夜 / 凌晨 1 点 —— 夏威夷和阿拉斯加。
⾼盛的 Rich Privorotsky 给出了⼀个简洁的参考框架：
缅因和阿拉斯加：可能拖到感恩节的悬念
如果参议院最终悬于⼀两席，缅因和阿拉斯加将成为市场最头疼的存在。
缅因州采⽤排序选择投票制，各市镇有两天时间上报官⽅计票结果，历史上该州通常在选举后
⼀周才能得出排序选择的结果，决选认证截⽌⽇期为 12 ⽉ 18 ⽇。
我们应该能很早就感知到⺠调误差的⽅向，但要得到参议院的官⽅结果，可能需要数天甚⾄数
周。
政治事件的出清应该是重要的。⾃ 1950 年以来，标普 500 在所有 19 次中期选举后的 12 个⽉内均
录得上涨 …… 没有任何保证，但僵局格局通常被视为对股市有利，尽管 AI 和数据中⼼相关限制仍
是需要关注的重要⻛险。第⼀道⻔槛是避免出现极端的中期选举结果 …… 德克萨斯将是重要的观
察窗⼝。

### Page 4

阿拉斯加州接受选举⽇后 15 天内从美国境外寄出的选票，该州历史上的紧张竞选⼏乎都会⽤满
这 15 天。此外，美国司法部已提起诉讼，若⺠主党候选⼈ Peltola 在 " 诱饵候选⼈ "Daniel J.
Sullivan 仍在选票上的情况下获胜，将阻⽌认证程序。
⾼盛的结论是：若参议院最终悬于阿拉斯加，华尔街可能要在联邦诉讼的阴影下，⼀直交易这
个⼆元结果到感恩节。
市场怎么定价不同结果
根据摩根⼤通市场情报部⻔的分析框架（概率为市场当前隐含值）：
66% 概率：⺠主党横扫 → 标普 500 当晚表现区间为下跌 0.25% ⾄上涨 0.5%
26% 概率：分裂国会 → 标普 500 上涨 0.5% ⾄ 1%
7% 概率：共和党横扫 → 标普 500 上涨 1% ⾄ 1.25%
也就是说，概率最⼤的结果，反⽽是对股市最不友好的结果 —— 尽管没有⼈预期当晚会出现暴
跌。
美银策略师 Michael Hartnett 则给出了更为悲观的⻛险评估。他将⺠主党全⾯横扫视为⼀个重⼤
的⻛险规避事件，并警告这⼀政治结果可能引发美股超过 10% 的调整⻛险。需要注意，这属于

极端情景下的⻛险判断。
债券市场的反应预计更为温和。瑞穗银⾏策略师 Evelyne Gomez-Liechti 估算，⺠主党横扫将使
10 年期美债收益率上升约 2 个基点（当⽇早间收益率为 4.91% ），分裂国会则将使其下⾏约 3 个基
点。但她同时警告，不要过度追逐 " 僵局意味着财政紧缩 " 的叙事：
特朗普愿意做交易，这可能导致国防⽀出上升，同时医疗补助削减计划被稀释，最终财政轨迹⼏

### Page 5

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

在具体交易层⾯，⾼盛篮⼦策略团队推荐的横扫交易是：做多 ACA （平价医疗法案）受益者篮
⼦。但⾏情出现了⼀个值得注意的背离：过去两周，该篮⼦已经回调，⽽预测市场的横扫概率
仍在攀升。⾼盛对此的判断是：要么篮⼦在提前反映某种预测市场尚未定价的信息，要么这是
⼀个低位介⼊的机会。
当下的核⼼问题
⾼盛的总结是：选举能否 " 成为出清事件并推动股市更⼴泛的⻛险重建 " ，取决于结果是否⾜够⼲
净。
但当前局⾯并不简单：众议院虽已是定局，参议院的多数席位却要穿越四个⺠主党领先幅度不
⾜ 3 点的共和党现任州，两个需要数⽇乃⾄数周才能完成计票的州，以及⼀个附带联邦诉讼的
州。
当下的第⼀个真正信号，来⾃美东时间晚 7:30 的俄亥俄州。
乎没有实质变化。

中國政府可能允許字節跳動及阿里巴巴等
大型中國科技企業購買一款輝達供高階 AI
推論與工作站應用設計的新晶片。 該晶片並
非專門針對大型資料中心訓練的高階 AI 晶
片，而未受美國現行出口管制規範 ， 在中國
方面雖尚無採購數量 、 時間表及審批標準的
具體訊息 ， 不過若中國政府放行企業採購管
制，為大型科技企業提供及時的硬體支撐，
或可視為美中晶片管制措施上具政策彈性 。

▋ 美中競逐發展超級智慧 
美中超級智慧熱線旨在建立直接溝通管道 ，
以應對駭客攻擊 、 國家安全疑慮或失控等潛
在問題， 但若回顧過往美中兩國熱線電話的
歷史，因美中文化或戰略歧異 (美方傾向即
時通話避免衝突升級 ， 中方高度集權使下屬
沒有取得最高領導人授權下不能表態 )而使
雙方在危機時刻並未能透過電話產生立即
性的有效溝通 。 市場期待美中雙方溝通仍有
待長期磨合並改採更可行(如文字溝通方式)
的對話機制。(圖二) 
此外 ， 市場分析美國可望於明年初步建立自
主稀土供應 ， 屆時可能恢復對中國施壓，並
將執行嚴格管制和封鎖 ， 美中將持續在尖端
科技上競爭 ， 並各自拉攏盟友推動全球形成
兩套不同的科技和供應鏈體系 。 即便美中在
超級智慧 發展上看似聯手風險控管又同時
互相掣肘下 ， 仍不改產業長線發展方向 ， 且
在各國普遍加碼投入 AI 發展下，瑞銀預估
全球 AI 資本支出今年上看 9,980 億美元，
明年支出規模將不斷攀升。(圖三)

圖二：歷史上美中熱線未達成即時有效溝通

撞 ， 美方試圖聯繫中方 ， 但初期無
法取得即時且有效的高層對話。

▋ 中國官方力推科技自主 
上半年中國全國人大四中全會批准並公佈
實施第十五個五年規劃綱要 ，將高水平科技
自立自強列為首要核心目標，規劃全社會研
發經費年均成長率維持超過 7%，以及先前
提出的新質生產力(以科技創新為主導的高
階生產力推動產業發展)均顯示政府推動科
技自主的戰略方向 ， 而美國在科技上的掣肘
措施 ， 更加刺激中國官方加速建立本土自主
可控的 AI 生態與供應鏈替代的政策方向，
以突破美國的晶片與技術禁令， 預期中國科
技產業發展仍將持續獲政策或補貼支持。

在 AI 基礎建設計畫方面，未來五年內中國
規劃編列 5 兆元人民幣投資於新型電網，
預計將拉動特高壓 、 智慧電網及配電網升級，
還有投入 2 兆元人民幣整合全國數據中心
資源，以實現算力統一調度等一系列基礎建
設計畫 ， 可望支援未來中國城市中自動駕駛 、
無人機和智慧城市的龐大算力需求。

受到美國持續收緊高階晶片出口管制 ，此次
建設中國官方明確指定至少 80%必須採用
國產晶片與技術設備，以提高中國 AI 晶片
自給率，中國本土半導體與 AI 晶片產業將
成為最大政策受益者，華為 、 寒武紀等中國
本土算力晶片可望加速技術迭代與商業化。
高盛預期中國國產AI 晶片出貨量將從2025
年 110 萬顆、2026 年 240 萬顆、2027 年
480 萬顆 ， 並攀升至 2030 年 1,100 萬顆 ，
市場規模將有望從人民幣 940 億元成長至
人民幣 6,460 億元。此外，中國伺服器、散
熱溫控等上下游產業鏈， 以及電網相關建設
廠商亦將受惠。(圖四)

圖四：中國國產 AI 晶片出貨量看增 
資料來源：GS，2026/9/26

▋ 結論 
美中在科技與政治上的角力 ，不但沒有阻礙
AI 產業的發展 ， 反而使競爭白熱化 ， 成為推
動中國加速技術突破與產業擴張的 強力催
化劑。為爭奪未來數十年的科技主導權 ，中
美雙方傾注國力提供政策支援或政府補貼
推動 AI 發展，民間企業如微軟、Google、
Meta、百度、騰訊、阿里等科技巨頭提出
史無前例的資金規模投入 AI 研發與算力基
礎設施。在晶片和演算法軍備競賽中 ， 中國
除了在發達的製造業領域 ， 還有自動駕駛 、
無人機等應用場景中全方位加速 AI 深度融
入，可望挹注中國科技業長線增長動能。

## Document 866: 韓国株が急落、AI・半導体好況の陰で市場の調整が深まる ～AI投資の先行き不安や米国の金利上昇、外国人投資家の売却圧力が重しに～

Source PDF: all report pdf/韓国株が急落、AI・半導体好況の陰で市場の調整が深まる ～AI投資の先行き不安や米国の金利上昇、外国人投資家の売却圧力が重しに～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-15
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年10 月 15 日

1/3 
韓国株が急落、AI・半導体好況の陰で市場の調整が深まる 
～AI 投資の先行き不安や米国の金利上昇、外国人投資家の売却圧力が重しに～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ 韓国総合株価指数 （KOSPI）は10 月 15 日まで７営業日連続で下落し、 半導体関連株を中心に
売りが広がるなか、６月半ばの最高値から４割以上下落した。一時はサーキットブレーカー
が発動する事態となったが、輸出や主要企業の業績は好調であり、株安は韓国経済の急激な
悪化よりも、ＡＩ関連投資への期待の見直しを反映している可能性がある。 
○ 背景には、巨額のＡＩ関連投資の収益性・持続可能性への懸念、中国半導体企業との競争激
化への警戒、米国のインフレ加速に伴う追加利上げ観測がある。さらに、外国人投資家の売
却が株価下落を増幅しており、国内投資家の買いでは吸収しきれていない。当局は市場安定
に向けた対応を強化する姿勢を示したものの、 海外投資家によるリスク回避の動きが続けば、
調整が長期化する可能性には注意が必要である。 
○ 旺盛なＡＩ・半導体需要は引き続き韓国経済を下支えする一方、株価の先行きは外部環境に
左右されやすい。エネルギー価格が高止まりするなか、ウォン安が進めば輸入インフレが強
まるほか、株安の長期化は家計消費や企業投資、政権支持率にも悪影響を及ぼしかねない。
足元の株価の急激な調整は、半導体関連産業への依存度の高さが、国際金融市場の変動に対
する脆弱性を強める構造的な問題を浮き彫りにしている。

### Page 2

2/3 
【韓国株は７営業日連続で下落、半導体関連株を中心に売り圧力が強まっている】 
韓国株式市場において急速な調整が進
んでいる （図１）。韓国総合株価指数
（KOSPI）は、10 月15 日に７営業日連続
の下落となったほか、 一時的に下落率が
９％を上回り、サーキットブレーカーが
発動して取引が 20 分間停止される事態
となった。特に、 時価総額上位のサムス
ン電子とＳＫハイニックス を中心とす
る半導体関連株で売り圧力が強まって
いる。 その結果、 足元の株価水準は２ヵ
月半ぶりの安値となり、 ６月半ばに記録
した最高値から４割以上も下落した格好となった。 
足元の株価下落を巡って注目されるのは、韓国経済のファンダメンタルズ（基礎的条件）が比較
的堅調であるにもかかわらず、株式市場では大幅な調整が進んでいることである。韓国では、世界
的なＡＩ （人工知能） ・ 半導体関連需要の旺盛さが輸出を押し上げており、 ９月の輸出額は前年比＋
83.5％と過去最高を更新した。なかでも半導体輸出は前年比＋262.8％と急拡大している。 さらに、
サムスン電子が 10 月８日に公表した７～９月期の暫定決算も過去最高を更新するなど、業績面で
は好調な動きが続いている。 それにもかかわらず、 同社をはじめとする半導体関連株が急落してい
ることは、 これまでの株価上昇を支えてきたＡＩ関連投資への期待が見直され始めている可能性を
示唆している。

【ＡＩ関連投資の収益性への懸念に加え、中国企業の技術力向上も警戒材料 となっている可能性】 
背景には、 米国を中心に進められてきた巨額のＡＩ関連投資が、 企業収益の持続的な拡大につな
がるかに関する不透明感が高まっていることがある。 世界的なＡＩ需要の拡大を受けて、 韓国の半
導体関連企業は旺盛な需要を取り込んできた。 しかし、 ＡＩ関連インフラへの投資規模が拡大する
なか、市場では、こうした投資の収益性や持続可能性に対する懸念が強まっている。加えて、中国
の半導体メーカーが、新たなメモリーセル構造を採用したサーバー向け DRAM を年内に投入する方
針を示しており、韓国の半導体関連株を下押しする要因となった。現時点においては、同社の量産
能力や歩留まり、 顧客による採用の見通しには不透明感が残るものの、 中国企業との競争激化が中
長期的にメモリー半導体の需給や価格形成に影響を及ぼすことが警戒される。 
さらに、 米国の金利動向を巡る不透明感も株価を下押ししている。米国の９月の消費者物価は前
年比＋3.7％と前月（同＋3.4％）から加速した。イラン情勢の悪化を受けた原油価格の高止まりが
インフレ圧力を強めており、米連邦準備制度理事会（ＦＲＢ）が追加利上げに動く可能性が改めて
意識されている。米国の金利が高止まりすれば、将来の高い利益成長を織り込んできたＡＩ・半導
体関連株には、株価評価の切り下げ圧力がかかりやすい。韓国は、株式市場において半導体関連企

### Page 3

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
3/3 
業の存在感が大きく、国際金融市場の動向に左右されやすい構造にある。このため、米国を発端と
する株価調整の動きや投資家のリスク回避姿勢の強まりが、 韓国株式市場に大きな影響を及ぼして
いると考えられる。

【外国人投資家の売りが調整を増幅、先行きも外部環境に左右される展開が続くか】 
こうしたなか、 外国人投資家による売却の動きが株価下落に拍車をかけている。韓国国内の個人
投資家のほか、機関投資家による買いの動きはみられるものの、 外国人投資家による売り圧力を吸
収するには至っていない。 さらに、 半導体関連企業による自社株買いの動きが縮小しつつあること
や、ＥＴＦ（上場投資信託）を巡る資金移動なども需給環境に影響を与えている模様である。韓国
の金融当局は 15 日に緊急会合を開催し、金融市場の安定に向けて対応を強化する姿勢を示したも
のの、 海外投資家のリスク回避姿勢が続けば、 株式市場の不安定な動きが長期化する可能性もある。 
先行きについては、韓国の半導体関連企業の業績が直ちに悪化するとは見込みにくいものの、株
価の調整が収束するかは不透明である。 世界的なＡＩ・半導体需要の旺盛さは韓国経済の下支え要
因である一方、 米国の金融政策を巡る不透明感や中国企業との競争激化への懸念が株価の重しとな
る可能性がある。さらに、イラン情勢の
悪化を背景にエネルギー価格が高止ま
りしており、７月以降堅調な動きをみせ
てきた ウォン相場 が下落に転じる動き
をみせれば （図２）、 輸入インフレを通じ
て国内の物価上昇圧力が一段と強まる
ことも懸念される。そのうえ、株価下落
が長期化すれば、 家計の資産効果や企業
の投資マインドを通じて実体経済に悪
影響が広がるほか、 政権支持率のさらな
る低迷を後押しすることも考えられる
（注１） 。このところの株価調整は、韓国経済の急激な悪化を直接的に意味するものではない もの
の、 半導体関連産業への依存度の高さが、 国際金融市場の変動に対する脆弱性を強める構造的な問
題を改めて浮き彫りにしたと捉えられる。
